#!/bin/bash
# build-deb.sh - Build Debian package with desktop integration
# Version: 1.1.1 (GUI wrapper, Web UI, and PNG icon included)

set -euo pipefail

VERSION="1.1.1"
PACKAGE_NAME="network-recover"
MAINTAINER="Network Recovery Team <support@network-recover.com>"
DESCRIPTION="Production-Grade Linux Network Recovery Engine - Diagnose and repair network issues without reboot"

# Colors

RED='\033[0;31m'

# Clean old build

mkdir -p "$PKG_DIR"

mkdir -p "$PKG_DIR/usr/local/bin"
mkdir -p "$PKG_DIR/usr/local/lib/network-recover"
mkdir -p "$PKG_DIR/usr/local/lib/network-recover/web"
mkdir -p "$PKG_DIR/usr/local/lib/network-recover/web/static"

 .
 Network Recovery Tool is the Linux equivalent of the Windows
 "Diagnose and Repair Network Problems" feature.
 .
 It provides structured, layered diagnostics and repairs for:
  - Physical layer (carrier, link state)
  - IP layer (IPv4, IPv6, duplicate detection)
  - Routing (default routes, metrics)
  - Gateway (ARP, reachability)
  - Internet (multiple targets, ICMP + TCP)
  - DNS (resolution, security checks)
  - HTTPS (actual browsing simulation)

#!/bin/bash
collect_iproute2() {
    echo "=== ip addr show ==="
    ip addr show 2>/dev/null
    echo ""
    echo "=== ip route show ==="
    ip route show 2>/dev/null
    echo ""
    echo "=== ip neigh show ==="
    ip neigh show 2>/dev/null
    echo ""
    echo "=== bridge link show ==="
    bridge link show 2>/dev/null || echo "  No bridges"
    return 0
}

#!/bin/bash
collect_journalctl() {
    echo "=== journalctl (NetworkManager last 50 lines) ==="
    journalctl -u NetworkManager -n 50 --no-pager 2>/dev/null || echo "  No NetworkManager logs available"
    return 0
}

#!/bin/bash
collect_libvirt() {
    if ! command -v virsh &>/dev/null; then
        echo "=== libvirt: not installed ==="
        return 0
    fi
    echo "=== virsh list --all ==="
    virsh list --all 2>/dev/null
    echo ""
    echo "=== virsh net-list --all ==="
    virsh net-list --all 2>/dev/null || echo "  No virtual networks"
    return 0
}

}

#!/bin/bash
check_bridge() {
    local bridge="${1:-$BRIDGE_IFACE}"
    local iface="${2:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}"
    local all_pass=true
    
    if [[ -z "$bridge" ]]; then
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  No bridge configured"
        return 0
    fi
    
    if ip link show "$bridge" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "UP"; then
        echo "  âœ… Bridge $bridge is UP"
    else
        echo "  âŒ Bridge $bridge is DOWN"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    if bridge link show "$bridge" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$iface"; then
        echo "  âœ… $iface enslaved to $bridge"
    else
        echo "  âŒ $iface NOT enslaved to $bridge"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

#!/bin/bash
check_dns() {
    local domain="${1:-google.com}"
    
    if nslookup "$domain" &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… DNS resolution works ($domain)"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ DNS resolution failed for $domain"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
check_gateway() {
    local gateway="${1:-$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $3; exit}' 2>/dev/null)}"
    
    if [[ -z "$gateway" ]]; then
        echo "  âŒ No gateway configured"
        return 1
    fi
    
    if ping -c 1 -W 2 "$gateway" &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… Gateway $gateway reachable"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ Gateway $gateway unreachable"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# diagnostics/https - HTTPS connectivity diagnostic with HTTP fallback
# Usage: check_https [url]

check_https() {
    local all_pass=true
    local https_urls=(
        "https://cloudflare.com"
        "https://google.com"
        "https://github.com"
    )
    local portal_url="http://connectivitycheck.gstatic.com/generate_204"
    
    # Allow override via argument
    if [[ -n "${1:-}" ]]; then
        https_urls=("$1")
    fi
    
    if ! command -v curl &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  curl not installed - HTTPS tests skipped"
        return 1
    fi
    
    echo "  ðŸ“Œ Testing HTTPS connectivity..."
    
    local success_count=0
    local total_urls=${#https_urls[@]}
    
    # Test HTTPS targets
    for url in "${https_urls[@]}"; do
        local http_code=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" \
            --connect-timeout 5 --max-time 10 "$url" 2>/dev/null)
        
        if [[ "$http_code" =~ ^[23][0-9][0-9]$ ]]; then
            echo "  âœ… HTTPS: $url (HTTP $http_code)"
            ((success_count++))
        elif [[ "$http_code" == "000" ]]; then
            echo "  âŒ HTTPS: $url (connection failed)"
            all_pass=false
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  HTTPS: $url (HTTP $http_code)"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    done
    
    # Always test for captive portal (can intercept even when some HTTPS works)
    echo ""
    echo "  ðŸ“Œ Testing for captive portal..."
    local portal_code=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" \
        --connect-timeout 5 --max-time 10 "$portal_url" 2>/dev/null)
    
    if [[ "$portal_code" == "204" ]]; then
        echo "  âœ… No captive portal detected"
    elif [[ "$portal_code" =~ ^[23][0-9][0-9]$ ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  Possible captive portal (HTTP $portal_code)"
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  You may need to log in to the network"
        all_pass=false
    elif [[ "$portal_code" == "000" ]]; then
        echo "  âŒ Captive portal test: connection failed"
        all_pass=false
    else
        echo "  âš ï¸  Captive portal test: unexpected response (HTTP $portal_code)"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Summary
    if [[ $success_count -ge 1 ]]; then
        echo "  âœ… HTTPS connectivity OK ($success_count/$total_urls)"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ HTTPS connectivity FAILED (0/$total_urls)"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# diagnostics/interface - interface layer diagnostic
# Usage: check_interface [iface]
# Enhanced with detailed interface information and production-ready output

check_interface() {
    local iface="${1:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}"
    local all_pass=true
    
    # Check if interface exists
    if [[ ! -d "/sys/class/net/$iface" ]]; then
        echo "  âŒ Interface $iface does not exist"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Gather interface details
    local operstate=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/operstate" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
    local mac=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/address" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
    local mtu=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/mtu" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
    local carrier=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/carrier" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    local speed=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/speed" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
    local duplex=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/duplex" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
    local flags=$(ip link show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'flags=\K[^ ]+' || echo "unknown")
    
    # Determine interface type
    local iface_type="unknown"
    if [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$iface/wireless" ]] || [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$iface/phy80211" ]]; then
        iface_type="wireless"
    elif [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$iface/bridge" ]]; then
        iface_type="bridge"
    elif [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$iface/bonding" ]]; then
        iface_type="bond"
    elif [[ "$iface" =~ ^(vnet|virbr) ]]; then
        iface_type="virtual"
    else
        iface_type="ethernet"
    fi
    
    # Display interface info with nice formatting
    echo "  â”Œâ”€ Interface: $iface"
    echo "  â”‚  Type: $iface_type"
    echo "  â”‚  MAC: $mac"
    echo "  â”‚  MTU: $mtu"
    echo "  â”‚  Flags: $flags"
    echo "  â”‚  State: $operstate"
    
    # Additional details based on interface type
    case "$iface_type" in
        wireless)
            local ssid=$(iwconfig "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'ESSID:"\K[^"]+' || echo "not connected")
            local signal=$(iwconfig "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'Signal level=\K[0-9-]+' || echo "unknown")
            local freq=$(iwconfig "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'Frequency:\K[0-9.]+ GHz' || echo "unknown")
            echo "  â”‚  SSID: $ssid"
            echo "  â”‚  Signal: ${signal} dBm"
            echo "  â”‚  Frequency: $freq"
            ;;
        bridge)
            local slaves=$(bridge link show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '^[0-9]+: \K[^:]+' | tr '\n' ', ' | sed 's/, $//')
            echo "  â”‚  Slaves: ${slaves:-none}"
            ;;
        ethernet)
            [[ "$speed" != "unknown" ]] && echo "  â”‚  Speed: $speed Mb/s"
            [[ "$duplex" != "unknown" ]] && echo "  â”‚  Duplex: $duplex"
            ;;
    esac
    
    echo "  â””â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€"
    
    # Check if carrier is present (link detected)
    if [[ "$carrier" == "1" ]]; then
        echo "  âœ… Carrier: present"
    else
        if [[ "$iface_type" == "wireless" ]]; then
            echo "  âš ï¸  Carrier: no WiFi connection"
        else
            echo "  âŒ Carrier: LOST - Check cable"
        fi
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check interface state
    if [[ "$operstate" == "up" ]]; then
        echo "  âœ… Interface state: $operstate"
    elif [[ "$operstate" == "dormant" ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  Interface state: $operstate (waiting for carrier)"
        all_pass=false
    else
        echo "  âŒ Interface state: $operstate (should be up)"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check if interface is UP from ip command
    if ip link show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "UP"; then
        echo "  âœ… Interface flags: UP"
    else
        echo "  âŒ Interface flags: DOWN"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check MTU sanity
    if [[ "$mtu" != "unknown" ]] && [[ "$mtu" -lt 1280 ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  MTU is very low ($mtu) - may cause issues"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check for interface errors
    local rx_errors=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/statistics/rx_errors" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    local tx_errors=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/statistics/tx_errors" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    if [[ "$rx_errors" != "0" ]] || [[ "$tx_errors" != "0" ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  Errors: RX=$rx_errors, TX=$tx_errors"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check for dropped packets
    local rx_dropped=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/statistics/rx_dropped" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    local tx_dropped=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/statistics/tx_dropped" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    if [[ "$rx_dropped" != "0" ]] || [[ "$tx_dropped" != "0" ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  Dropped: RX=$rx_dropped, TX=$tx_dropped"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check for collisions
    local collisions=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/statistics/collisions" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    if [[ "$collisions" != "0" ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  Collisions: $collisions"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    echo ""
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

#!/bin/bash
check_ip() {
    local iface="${1:-${BRIDGE_IFACE:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}}"
    
    local ipv4=$(ip -4 addr show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '(?<=inet\s)\d+(\.\d+){3}/\d+' | head -1)
    
    if [[ -n "$ipv4" ]]; then
        echo "  âœ… IPv4: $ipv4"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ No IPv4 address on $iface"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
check_kvm() {
    if ! command -v virsh &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  libvirt not installed"
        return 0
    fi
    
    if systemctl is-active libvirtd &>/dev/null; then
        local running=$(virsh list --state-running --name 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        local total=$(virsh list --all --name 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        echo "  âœ… libvirtd running - $running/$total VMs active"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âš ï¸  libvirtd not running"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
check_networkmanager() {
    if ! command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  NetworkManager not installed"
        return 0
    fi
    
    if systemctl is-active NetworkManager &>/dev/null; then
        local state=$(nmcli -t -f GENERAL.STATE general status 2>/dev/null | cut -d: -f2)
        echo "  âœ… NetworkManager is running (state: ${state:-unknown})"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ NetworkManager is NOT running"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# diagnostics/physical - physical layer diagnostic
# Usage: check_physical [iface]
# Enhanced with detailed physical layer information

check_physical() {
    local all_pass=true
    local iface="${1:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}"
    
    echo "  â”Œâ”€ Physical Layer: $iface"
    
    # Check if interface exists
    if [[ ! -d "/sys/class/net/$iface" ]]; then
        echo "  â”‚  âŒ Interface does not exist"
        echo "  â””â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Determine interface type
    local iface_type="unknown"
    if [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$iface/wireless" ]] || [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$iface/phy80211" ]]; then
        iface_type="wireless"
    elif [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$iface/bridge" ]]; then
        iface_type="bridge"
    else
        iface_type="ethernet"
    fi
    echo "  â”‚  Type: $iface_type"
    
    # Check carrier (link detection)
    if [[ -f "/sys/class/net/$iface/carrier" ]]; then
        local carrier=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/carrier")
        if [[ "$carrier" == "1" ]]; then
            echo "  â”‚  âœ… Carrier: present"
        else
            if [[ "$iface_type" == "wireless" ]]; then
                echo "  â”‚  âš ï¸  Carrier: no WiFi connection"
            else
                echo "  â”‚  âŒ Carrier: LOST - Check cable"
            fi
            all_pass=false
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check operstate
    if [[ -f "/sys/class/net/$iface/operstate" ]]; then
        local state=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/operstate")
        case "$state" in
            up)
                echo "  â”‚  âœ… State: $state"
                ;;
            down)
                echo "  â”‚  âŒ State: $state (interface is down)"
                all_pass=false
                ;;
            dormant)
                echo "  â”‚  âš ï¸  State: $state (waiting for carrier)"
                all_pass=false
                ;;
            *)
                echo "  â”‚  âŒ State: $state (unexpected)"
                all_pass=false
                ;;
        esac
    fi
    
    # Check link speed (ethernet only)
    if [[ "$iface_type" == "ethernet" ]] && command -v ethtool &>/dev/null; then
        if [[ -e "/sys/class/net/$iface/device" ]]; then
            local speed=$(timeout 2 ethtool "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -i "Speed:" | awk '{print $2}')
            local duplex=$(timeout 2 ethtool "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -i "Duplex:" | awk '{print $2}')
            local link=$(timeout 2 ethtool "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -i "Link detected:" | awk '{print $3}')
            
            if [[ -n "$speed" ]] && [[ "$speed" != "Unknown!" ]]; then
                echo "  â”‚  Speed: $speed"
            fi
            if [[ -n "$duplex" ]] && [[ "$duplex" != "Unknown!" ]]; then
                echo "  â”‚  Duplex: $duplex"
            fi
            if [[ -n "$link" ]]; then
                if [[ "$link" == "yes" ]]; then
                    echo "  â”‚  âœ… Link detected: yes"
                else
                    echo "  â”‚  âŒ Link detected: no"
                    all_pass=false
                fi
            fi
        fi
    fi
    
    # WiFi signal strength
    if [[ "$iface_type" == "wireless" ]] && command -v iwconfig &>/dev/null; then
        local signal=$(iwconfig "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'Signal level=\K[0-9-]+' || echo "")
        local quality=$(iwconfig "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'Quality=\K[0-9/]+' || echo "")
        if [[ -n "$signal" ]]; then
            echo "  â”‚  Signal: ${signal} dBm"
            # Interpret signal strength
            if [[ "$signal" -gt -50 ]]; then
                echo "  â”‚  ðŸ“¶ Excellent"
            elif [[ "$signal" -gt -60 ]]; then
                echo "  â”‚  ðŸ“¶ Good"
            elif [[ "$signal" -gt -70 ]]; then
                echo "  â”‚  ðŸ“¶ Fair"
            else
                echo "  â”‚  ðŸ“¶ Poor"
            fi
        fi
        if [[ -n "$quality" ]]; then
            echo "  â”‚  Quality: $quality"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check for interface errors
    local rx_errors=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/statistics/rx_errors" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    local tx_errors=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$iface/statistics/tx_errors" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    if [[ "$rx_errors" != "0" ]] || [[ "$tx_errors" != "0" ]]; then
        echo "  â”‚  âš ï¸  Errors: RX=$rx_errors, TX=$tx_errors"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    echo "  â””â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€"
    echo ""
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

#!/bin/bash
check_routing() {
    local gateway=$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $3; exit}' 2>/dev/null)
    
    if [[ -n "$gateway" ]]; then
        echo "  âœ… Default gateway: $gateway"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ No default route found"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
#===============================================================================
# Network Recovery Tool - Installer
# Version: 1.1.2
# Installs the tool, GUI wrapper, web interface, modular components, 
# XFCE integration, icons, polkit rules, and Python dependencies
#===============================================================================

set -euo pipefail

readonly APP_VERSION="1.1.2"
readonly SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
readonly BIN_DIR="/usr/local/bin"
readonly LIB_DIR="/usr/local/lib/network-recover"
readonly APP_DIR="/usr/share/applications"
readonly POLKIT_DIR="/usr/share/polkit-1/actions"
readonly LOG_DIR="/var/log/network-events"
readonly SNAPSHOT_DIR="/var/lib/network-recover/snapshots"

# Colors
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
NC='\033[0m'

# Parse arguments
UNINSTALL=false
NO_GUI=false
SKIP_DEPS=false

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
    case $1 in
        --uninstall|-u)
            UNINSTALL=true
            shift
            ;;
        --no-gui)
            NO_GUI=true
            shift
            ;;
        --skip-deps)
            SKIP_DEPS=true
            shift
            ;;
        --help|-h)
            echo "Network Recovery Tool Installer v$APP_VERSION"
            echo ""
            echo "Usage: sudo ./install.sh [OPTIONS]"
            echo ""
            echo "Options:"
            echo "  --uninstall, -u   Uninstall the tool"
            echo "  --no-gui          Skip GUI dependencies (headless server)"
            echo "  --skip-deps       Skip dependency installation"
            echo "  --help, -h        Show this help"
            echo ""
            echo "Examples:"
            echo "  sudo ./install.sh              # Install"
            echo "  sudo ./install.sh --uninstall  # Uninstall"
            echo "  sudo ./install.sh --no-gui     # Install without GUI"
            exit 0
            ;;
        *)
            echo "Unknown option: $1"
            echo "Use --help for usage"
            exit 1
            ;;
    esac
done

#===============================================================================
# UNINSTALL
#===============================================================================

if [[ "$UNINSTALL" == "true" ]]; then
    echo ""
    echo "=============================================="
    echo " Network Recovery Tool v$APP_VERSION - Uninstaller"
    echo "=============================================="
    echo ""
    
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Removing binaries..."
    rm -f "$BIN_DIR/network-recover" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $BIN_DIR/network-recover" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found: $BIN_DIR/network-recover"
    rm -f "$BIN_DIR/network-recover-gui" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $BIN_DIR/network-recover-gui" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found: $BIN_DIR/network-recover-gui"
    rm -f "$BIN_DIR/network-recover-web" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $BIN_DIR/network-recover-web" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found: $BIN_DIR/network-recover-web"
    rm -f "$BIN_DIR/network-recover-dashboard" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $BIN_DIR/network-recover-dashboard" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found: $BIN_DIR/network-recover-dashboard"
    
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Removing desktop entry..."
    rm -f "$APP_DIR/network-recover.desktop" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $APP_DIR/network-recover.desktop" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found"
    
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Removing polkit policy..."
    rm -f "$POLKIT_DIR/com.network-recover.policy" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $POLKIT_DIR/com.network-recover.policy" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found"
    
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Removing modules..."
    rm -rf "$LIB_DIR" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $LIB_DIR" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found"
    
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Removing icons..."
    rm -f "/usr/share/icons/hicolor/scalable/apps/network-recover.svg" 2>/dev/null || true
    rm -f "/usr/share/icons/hicolor/48x48/apps/network-recover.png" 2>/dev/null || true
    echo "  âœ… Icons removed"
    
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Removing logs and snapshots..."
    rm -rf "$LOG_DIR" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $LOG_DIR" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found"
    rm -rf "$SNAPSHOT_DIR" && echo "  âœ… Removed: $SNAPSHOT_DIR" || echo "  âš ï¸  Not found"
    
    # Clear XFCE panel cache
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Clearing XFCE panel cache..."
    rm -rf /root/.cache/xfce4/ 2>/dev/null || true
    for user_home in /home/*; do
        if [[ -d "$user_home" ]]; then
            rm -rf "$user_home/.cache/xfce4/" 2>/dev/null || true
        fi
    done
    echo "  âœ… Panel cache cleared"
    
    echo ""
    echo "=============================================="
    echo "  âœ… UNINSTALLATION COMPLETE!"
    echo "=============================================="
    exit 0
fi

#===============================================================================
# MAIN INSTALLATION
#===============================================================================

echo ""
echo "=============================================="
echo " Network Recovery Tool v$APP_VERSION - Installer"
echo "=============================================="
echo ""

# Check root
if [[ $EUID -ne 0 ]]; then
    echo -e "${RED}âŒ This installer must be run as root (sudo)${NC}"
    exit 1
fi

# Detect distribution
detect_distro() {
    if [[ -f /etc/os-release ]]; then
        . /etc/os-release
        echo "$ID"
    else
        echo "unknown"
    fi
}

DISTRO=$(detect_distro)
echo -e "${GREEN}âœ… Detected distribution: $DISTRO${NC}"

#===============================================================================
# STEP 1: Install system dependencies
#===============================================================================
if [[ "$SKIP_DEPS" != "true" ]]; then
    echo ""
    echo "ðŸ“¦ Installing system dependencies..."
    
    install_deps_debian() {
        apt-get update -qq
        local deps="curl iproute2 bridge-utils ethtool netcat-openbsd dnsutils network-manager policykit-1 python3 python3-pip"
        if [[ "$NO_GUI" != "true" ]]; then
            deps="$deps zenity libnotify-bin"
        fi
        apt-get install -y -qq $deps 2>/dev/null || true
    }
    
    install_deps_rhel() {
        local deps="curl iproute bridge-utils ethtool nmap-ncat bind-utils NetworkManager polkit python3 python3-pip"
        if [[ "$NO_GUI" != "true" ]]; then
            deps="$deps zenity libnotify"
        fi
        yum install -y -q $deps 2>/dev/null || true
    }
    
    install_deps_arch() {
        local deps="curl iproute2 bridge-utils ethtool openbsd-netcat bind networkmanager polkit python python-pip"
        if [[ "$NO_GUI" != "true" ]]; then
            deps="$deps zenity libnotify"
        fi
        pacman -S --noconfirm --needed $deps 2>/dev/null || true
    }
    
    case "$DISTRO" in
        debian|ubuntu|mx|linuxmint|pop)
            install_deps_debian
            ;;
        fedora|rhel|centos|rocky|alma)
            install_deps_rhel
            ;;
        arch|manjaro|endeavouros)
            install_deps_arch
            ;;
        *)
            echo -e "${YELLOW}âš ï¸  Unknown distribution - please install dependencies manually:${NC}"
            echo "   core: curl, iproute2, bridge-utils, ethtool, netcat, dnsutils, network-manager, polkit, python3, python3-pip"
            if [[ "$NO_GUI" != "true" ]]; then
                echo "   gui:  zenity, libnotify"
            fi
            ;;
    esac
    
    echo -e "${GREEN}âœ… System dependencies installed${NC}"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 1.5: Install Python dependencies for web server
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    echo ""
    echo "ðŸ Installing Python dependencies..."

    py_module_available() {
        python3 -c "import $1" 2>/dev/null && return 0 || return 1
    }

    PYTHON_DEPS_MISSING=false

    # --- psutil ---
    if py_module_available "psutil"; then
        echo "  âœ… psutil already installed"
    else
        echo "  ðŸ“¦ Installing psutil..."
        PSUTIL_INSTALLED=false
        
        case "$DISTRO" in
            debian|ubuntu|mx|linuxmint|pop)
                apt-get install -y -qq python3-psutil 2>/dev/null && PSUTIL_INSTALLED=true
                ;;
            fedora|rhel|centos|rocky|alma)
                dnf install -y python3-psutil 2>/dev/null && PSUTIL_INSTALLED=true
                ;;
            arch|manjaro|endeavouros)
                pacman -S --noconfirm python-psutil 2>/dev/null && PSUTIL_INSTALLED=true
                ;;
        esac
        
        if [[ "$PSUTIL_INSTALLED" != "true" ]]; then
            pip3 install psutil 2>/dev/null && PSUTIL_INSTALLED=true
        fi
        
        if py_module_available "psutil"; then
            echo "  âœ… psutil installed successfully"
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  psutil installation failed - VM metrics will be limited"
            PYTHON_DEPS_MISSING=true
        fi
    fi

    # --- websockets ---
    if py_module_available "websockets"; then
        echo "  âœ… websockets already installed"
    else
        echo "  ðŸ“¦ Installing websockets..."
        WEBSOCKETS_INSTALLED=false
        
        case "$DISTRO" in
            debian|ubuntu|mx|linuxmint|pop)
                apt-get install -y -qq python3-websockets 2>/dev/null && WEBSOCKETS_INSTALLED=true
                ;;
            fedora|rhel|centos|rocky|alma)
                dnf install -y python3-websockets 2>/dev/null && WEBSOCKETS_INSTALLED=true
                ;;
            arch|manjaro|endeavouros)
                pacman -S --noconfirm python-websockets 2>/dev/null && WEBSOCKETS_INSTALLED=true
                ;;
        esac
        
        if [[ "$WEBSOCKETS_INSTALLED" != "true" ]]; then
            pip3 install websockets 2>/dev/null && WEBSOCKETS_INSTALLED=true
        fi
        
        if py_module_available "websockets"; then
            echo "  âœ… websockets installed successfully"
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  websockets installation failed - real-time updates disabled"
            PYTHON_DEPS_MISSING=true
        fi
    fi

    echo ""
    if [[ "$PYTHON_DEPS_MISSING" == "true" ]]; then
        echo -e "${YELLOW}âš ï¸  Some Python dependencies are missing.${NC}"
    else
        echo -e "${GREEN}âœ… All Python dependencies installed${NC}"
    fi

else
    echo -e "${YELLOW}âš ï¸  Skipping dependency installation${NC}"
fi

#===============================================================================
# STEP 2: Create directories
#===============================================================================
echo ""
echo "ðŸ“ Creating directories..."
mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR" "$SNAPSHOT_DIR"
chmod 755 "$LOG_DIR" "$SNAPSHOT_DIR"
echo -e "${GREEN}âœ… Directories created${NC}"

#===============================================================================
# STEP 3: Install core engine
#===============================================================================
echo ""
echo "ðŸ“‹ Installing core engine..."
if [[ -f "$SCRIPT_DIR/src/network-recover" ]]; then
    cp "$SCRIPT_DIR/src/network-recover" "$BIN_DIR/network-recover"
    chmod 755 "$BIN_DIR/network-recover"
    echo -e "${GREEN}âœ… Core engine installed to $BIN_DIR/network-recover${NC}"
else
    echo -e "${RED}âŒ src/network-recover not found!${NC}"
    exit 1
fi

#===============================================================================
# STEP 4: Install GUI wrapper
#===============================================================================
if [[ "$NO_GUI" != "true" ]]; then
    echo ""
    echo "ðŸ–¥ï¸  Installing GUI wrapper..."
    if [[ -f "$SCRIPT_DIR/src/network-recover-gui" ]]; then
        cp "$SCRIPT_DIR/src/network-recover-gui" "$BIN_DIR/network-recover-gui"
        chmod 755 "$BIN_DIR/network-recover-gui"
        echo -e "${GREEN}âœ… GUI wrapper installed to $BIN_DIR/network-recover-gui${NC}"

    fi

fi

echo ""

# STEP 8: XFCE panel integration

echo ""
echo "ðŸ”§ Setting up XFCE panel integration..."

setup_xfce_panel() {
    mkdir -p /home/*/.local/share/applications 2>/dev/null || true
    mkdir -p /root/.local/share/applications 2>/dev/null || true
    
    cp "$APP_DIR/network-recover.desktop" /etc/skel/.local/share/applications/ 2>/dev/null || true
    
    for user_home in /home/*; do
        if [[ -d "$user_home" ]]; then
            mkdir -p "$user_home/.local/share/applications"
            cp "$APP_DIR/network-recover.desktop" "$user_home/.local/share/applications/"
            chown -R "$(basename "$user_home"):$(basename "$user_home")" "$user_home/.local/share/applications/" 2>/dev/null || true
        fi
    done
    
    if pgrep -x "xfce4-panel" > /dev/null; then
        echo -e "${GREEN}âœ… XFCE panel detected - launcher available in panel preferences${NC}"
        echo ""
        echo "   To add to your panel right now:"
        echo "   1. Right-click panel â†’ Panel â†’ Add New Items"
        echo "   2. Search for 'Network Recovery Tool'"
        echo "   3. Click 'Add'"
        echo ""
        xfce4-panel --add=launcher 2>/dev/null || true
    else
        echo -e "${YELLOW}âš ï¸  XFCE panel not running - launcher will appear after next login${NC}"
    fi
}

setup_xfce_panel

    for user_home in /home/*; do
        if [[ -d "$user_home" ]]; then

        fi
    done
    xfce4-panel &

else
    echo "  â„¹ï¸  XFCE panel not running - cache will refresh on next login"

#!/bin/bash
#===============================================================================
# XFCE Panel Integration for Network Recovery Tool
# Generic version - works on any XFCE panel configuration
#===============================================================================

echo "ðŸ”§ Setting up Network Recovery panel integration..."

# Check if XFCE panel is running
if ! pgrep -x "xfce4-panel" > /dev/null; then
    echo "âš ï¸  XFCE panel not running - integration will apply on next login"
fi

# Ensure desktop file exists
if [[ ! -f /usr/share/applications/network-recover.desktop ]]; then
    echo "âŒ Desktop file not found. Run the installer first."
    exit 1
fi

# Copy to user's local applications
mkdir -p ~/.local/share/applications
cp /usr/share/applications/network-recover.desktop ~/.local/share/applications/

# Try to add launcher to the panel
if command -v xfconf-query &>/dev/null; then
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Adding launcher to XFCE panel..."
    
    # Detect ALL panels
    PANELS=$(xfconf-query -c xfce4-panel -p /panels -l 2>/dev/null | grep -E '^/panels/panel-[0-9]+$' | sed 's|/panels/||')
    
    if [[ -z "$PANELS" ]]; then
        echo "âš ï¸  No XFCE panels detected"
    else
        # Find which panel has the systray (network icon)
        TARGET_PANEL=""
        SYSTRAY_PLUGIN=""
        SYSTRAY_POS=""
        PLUGIN_LIST=""
        
        for panel in $PANELS; do
            PLUGIN_LIST=$(xfconf-query -c xfce4-panel -p /panels/$panel/plugin-ids 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[]' | tr -d ' ')
            
            if [[ -n "$PLUGIN_LIST" ]]; then
                IFS=',' read -ra PLUGINS <<< "$PLUGIN_LIST"
                
                # Check if any plugin in this panel is a systray or statusnotifier
                for plugin_id in "${PLUGINS[@]}"; do
                    PLUGIN_TYPE=$(xfconf-query -c xfce4-panel -p /plugins/plugin-$plugin_id/type 2>/dev/null)
                    
                    if [[ "$PLUGIN_TYPE" == "systray" ]] || [[ "$PLUGIN_TYPE" == "statusnotifier" ]]; then
                        TARGET_PANEL="$panel"
                        SYSTRAY_PLUGIN="$plugin_id"
                        # Find position
                        for i in "${!PLUGINS[@]}"; do
                            if [[ "${PLUGINS[$i]}" == "$plugin_id" ]]; then
                                SYSTRAY_POS=$i
                                break
                            fi
                        done
                        break 2
                    fi
                done
            fi
        done
        
        if [[ -z "$TARGET_PANEL" ]]; then
            echo "âš ï¸  No systray/statusnotifier plugin found (network icon not detected)"
            echo "   The launcher will be added to the first panel instead."
            
            # Fallback: use first panel
            TARGET_PANEL=$(echo "$PANELS" | head -1)
            PLUGIN_LIST=$(xfconf-query -c xfce4-panel -p /panels/$TARGET_PANEL/plugin-ids 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[]' | tr -d ' ')
            SYSTRAY_POS=-1  # Add to end
        fi
        
        # Find next available plugin ID
        if [[ -n "$PLUGIN_LIST" ]]; then
            IFS=',' read -ra PLUGINS <<< "$PLUGIN_LIST"
            NEXT_ID=$(printf "%s\n" "${PLUGINS[@]}" | sort -n | tail -1)
            NEXT_ID=$((NEXT_ID + 1))
        else
            NEXT_ID=1
        fi
        
        # Create new launcher plugin
        xfconf-query -c xfce4-panel -p /plugins/plugin-$NEXT_ID -n -t string -s "launcher" 2>/dev/null || true
        xfconf-query -c xfce4-panel -p /plugins/plugin-$NEXT_ID/items -n -t string -s "network-recover.desktop" 2>/dev/null || true
        
        # Build new plugin list with launcher inserted after systray
        if [[ -n "$PLUGIN_LIST" ]]; then
            IFS=',' read -ra PLUGINS <<< "$PLUGIN_LIST"
            NEW_LIST=""
            
            if [[ "$SYSTRAY_POS" -ge 0 ]]; then
                # Insert after systray
                for i in "${!PLUGINS[@]}"; do
                    NEW_LIST="${NEW_LIST}${PLUGINS[$i]},"
                    if [[ $i -eq $SYSTRAY_POS ]]; then
                        NEW_LIST="${NEW_LIST}${NEXT_ID},"
                    fi
                done
            else
                # Add to end
                for i in "${!PLUGINS[@]}"; do
                    NEW_LIST="${NEW_LIST}${PLUGINS[$i]},"
                done
                NEW_LIST="${NEW_LIST}${NEXT_ID},"
            fi
            
            # Remove trailing comma and wrap in brackets
            NEW_LIST="[${NEW_LIST%,}]"
            
            # Apply new plugin order
            xfconf-query -c xfce4-panel -p /panels/$TARGET_PANEL/plugin-ids -s "$NEW_LIST" 2>/dev/null || true
            
            if [[ "$SYSTRAY_POS" -ge 0 ]]; then
                echo "âœ… Launcher added to panel $TARGET_PANEL (plugin $NEXT_ID) next to network icon"
            else
                echo "âœ… Launcher added to panel $TARGET_PANEL (plugin $NEXT_ID)"
            fi
        else
            echo "âš ï¸  Could not determine plugin list"
        fi
    fi
    
    # If automated addition failed, show manual instructions
    if [[ -z "$TARGET_PANEL" ]] || [[ -z "$NEXT_ID" ]]; then
        echo ""
        echo "   To add manually:"
        echo "   1. Right-click panel â†’ Panel â†’ Panel Preferences"
        echo "   2. Click 'Items' tab"
        echo "   3. Click '+' Add"
        echo "   4. Search for 'Network Diagnose & Repair'"
        echo "   5. Click 'Add'"
        echo "   6. Drag it next to the network icon (usually in Status Tray)"
        echo ""
    fi
else
    echo "âš ï¸  xfconf-query not found - cannot auto-configure panel"
    echo ""
    echo "   Please add the launcher manually:"
    echo "   1. Right-click panel â†’ Panel â†’ Panel Preferences"
    echo "   2. Click 'Items' tab"
    echo "   3. Click '+' Add"
    echo "   4. Search for 'Network Diagnose & Repair'"
    echo "   5. Click 'Add'"
    echo "   6. Drag it next to the network icon"
    echo ""
fi

# Restart panel to apply changes
if pgrep -x "xfce4-panel" > /dev/null; then
    echo "ðŸ”„ Restarting XFCE panel..."
    xfce4-panel -r 2>/dev/null || pkill -USR1 xfce4-panel 2>/dev/null || true
    echo "âœ… Panel restarted"
fi

echo ""
echo "=========================================="
echo "  PANEL INTEGRATION COMPLETE"
echo "=========================================="
echo ""
echo "  Your icon should now appear next to"
echo "  the network icon in your panel."
echo ""
echo "  If not, add it manually using the"
echo "  instructions above."
echo ""

# Network Recovery Tool Makefile
# Version: 1.1.1

VERSION = 1.1.1
PREFIX ?= /usr/local
BINDIR = $(PREFIX)/bin
LIBDIR = $(PREFIX)/lib/network-recover
DESKTOPDIR = $(PREFIX)/share/applications
POLKITDIR = $(PREFIX)/share/polkit-1/actions
ICONDIR = $(PREFIX)/share/icons/hicolor
LOGDIR = /var/log/network-events
SNAPSHOTDIR = /var/lib/network-recover/snapshots

# Colors for output
GREEN = \033[0;32m
RED = \033[0;31m
YELLOW = \033[1;33m
NC = \033[0m

.PHONY: all install uninstall test help

all: install

install:
	@echo "Installing network-recover v$(VERSION)..."
	@echo "  PREFIX: $(PREFIX)"
	@echo ""
	
	# Create directories
	@mkdir -p $(BINDIR) $(LIBDIR) $(DESKTOPDIR) $(POLKITDIR) $(LOGDIR) $(SNAPSHOTDIR)
	@echo "  ✅ Directories created"
	
	# Install core engine
	@if [ -f src/network-recover ]; then \
		install -m 755 src/network-recover $(BINDIR)/ && \
		echo "  ✅ Core engine: $(BINDIR)/network-recover"; \
	else \
		echo "  ❌ src/network-recover not found"; \
		exit 1; \
	fi
	
	# Install GUI wrapper
	@if [ -f src/network-recover-gui ]; then \
		install -m 755 src/network-recover-gui $(BINDIR)/ && \
		echo "  ✅ GUI wrapper: $(BINDIR)/network-recover-gui"; \
	else \
		echo "  ⚠️  src/network-recover-gui not found (optional)"; \
	fi
	
	# Install desktop file
	@if [ -f desktop/network-recover.desktop ]; then \
		install -m 644 desktop/network-recover.desktop $(DESKTOPDIR)/ && \
		echo "  ✅ Desktop entry: $(DESKTOPDIR)/network-recover.desktop"; \
	else \
		echo "  ⚠️  desktop/network-recover.desktop not found"; \
	fi
	
	# Install modular components
	@if [ -d diagnostics ]; then \
		cp -r diagnostics $(LIBDIR)/ && \
		chmod -R 755 $(LIBDIR)/diagnostics && \
		echo "  ✅ diagnostics/ installed ($(shell ls -1 diagnostics 2>/dev/null | wc -l) modules)"; \
	fi
	
	@if [ -d repairs ]; then \
		cp -r repairs $(LIBDIR)/ && \
		chmod -R 755 $(LIBDIR)/repairs && \
		echo "  ✅ repairs/ installed ($(shell ls -1 repairs 2>/dev/null | wc -l) modules)"; \
	fi
	
	@if [ -d collectors ]; then \
		cp -r collectors $(LIBDIR)/ && \
		chmod -R 755 $(LIBDIR)/collectors && \
		echo "  ✅ collectors/ installed ($(shell ls -1 collectors 2>/dev/null | wc -l) modules)"; \
	fi
	
	# Install icons
	@if [ -d icons ]; then \
		mkdir -p $(ICONDIR)/scalable/apps; \
		mkdir -p $(ICONDIR)/48x48/apps; \
		if [ -f icons/network-recover.svg ]; then \
			install -m 644 icons/network-recover.svg $(ICONDIR)/scalable/apps/; \
			echo "  ✅ SVG icon installed"; \
		fi; \
		if [ -f icons/network-recover.png ]; then \
			install -m 644 icons/network-recover.png $(ICONDIR)/48x48/apps/; \
			echo "  ✅ PNG icon installed"; \
		fi; \
		update-icon-caches $(ICONDIR) 2>/dev/null || true; \
	fi
	
	# Install polkit policy
	@if [ -f polkit/com.network-recover.policy ]; then \
		install -m 644 polkit/com.network-recover.policy $(POLKITDIR)/ && \
		echo "  ✅ Polkit policy: $(POLKITDIR)/com.network-recover.policy"; \
	else \
		echo "  ⚠️  polkit/com.network-recover.policy not found"; \
	fi
	
	# Update desktop database
	@update-desktop-database $(DESKTOPDIR) 2>/dev/null || true
	
	@echo ""
	@echo "=============================================="
	@echo "  ✅ INSTALLATION COMPLETE!"
	@echo "=============================================="
	@echo ""
	@echo "  Quick commands:"
	@echo "    sudo network-recover diagnose"
	@echo "    sudo network-recover repair"
	@echo "    sudo network-recover status"
	@echo "    sudo network-recover snapshot"
	@echo "    sudo network-recover watch"
	@echo ""
	@echo "  Panel integration:"
	@echo "    sudo ./integration/xfce-integration.sh"
	@echo ""

uninstall:
	@echo "Uninstalling network-recover v$(VERSION)..."
	@echo ""
	
	# Remove binaries
	@rm -f $(BINDIR)/network-recover && echo "  ✅ Removed: $(BINDIR)/network-recover" || echo "  ⚠️  Not found: $(BINDIR)/network-recover"
	@rm -f $(BINDIR)/network-recover-gui && echo "  ✅ Removed: $(BINDIR)/network-recover-gui" || echo "  ⚠️  Not found: $(BINDIR)/network-recover-gui"
	
	# Remove desktop file
	@rm -f $(DESKTOPDIR)/network-recover.desktop && echo "  ✅ Removed: $(DESKTOPDIR)/network-recover.desktop" || echo "  ⚠️  Not found: $(DESKTOPDIR)/network-recover.desktop"
	
	# Remove polkit policy
	@rm -f $(POLKITDIR)/com.network-recover.policy && echo "  ✅ Removed: $(POLKITDIR)/com.network-recover.policy" || echo "  ⚠️  Not found: $(POLKITDIR)/com.network-recover.policy"
	
	# Remove modules
	@rm -rf $(LIBDIR) && echo "  ✅ Removed: $(LIBDIR)" || echo "  ⚠️  Not found: $(LIBDIR)"
	
	# Remove icons
	@rm -f $(ICONDIR)/scalable/apps/network-recover.svg 2>/dev/null || true
	@rm -f $(ICONDIR)/48x48/apps/network-recover.png 2>/dev/null || true
	@echo "  ✅ Icons removed"
	
	# Remove logs and snapshots (ask confirmation)
	@echo "  ⚠️  Removing logs and snapshots:"
	@rm -rf $(LOGDIR) && echo "  ✅ Removed: $(LOGDIR)" || echo "  ⚠️  Not found: $(LOGDIR)"
	@rm -rf $(SNAPSHOTDIR) && echo "  ✅ Removed: $(SNAPSHOTDIR)" || echo "  ⚠️  Not found: $(SNAPSHOTDIR)"
	
	# Update desktop database
	@update-desktop-database $(DESKTOPDIR) 2>/dev/null || true
	
	@echo ""
	@echo "=============================================="
	@echo "  ✅ UNINSTALLATION COMPLETE!"
	@echo "=============================================="

test:
	@echo "Running tests..."
	@bash tests/test-manual.sh

help:
	@echo "Network Recovery Tool Makefile"
	@echo ""
	@echo "  make install   - Install the tool"
	@echo "  make uninstall - Remove the tool"
	@echo "  make test      - Run tests"
	@echo "  make help      - Show this help"
	@echo ""
	@echo "Variables:"
	@echo "  PREFIX=$(PREFIX)  - Installation prefix"
	@echo "  VERSION=$(VERSION) - Tool version"

# Linux Network Recovery Tool

[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-yellow.svg)](LICENSE)
[![Version](https://img.shields.io/badge/version-1.1.1-blue)](https://github.com/cypso05/Linux_Network-Recovery-Tool/releases)
[![Platform](https://img.shields.io/badge/platform-Linux-brightgreen)]()

A diagnostic and repair utility for Linux network connectivity issues. Designed to solve the "connected but cannot browse" problem without requiring a system reboot.

---

## What This Tool Does

When NetworkManager shows "Connected" but web pages won't load, this tool runs a structured series of checks — from the physical interface up through DNS resolution and HTTPS connectivity. If it finds a problem, it applies targeted repairs.

It wraps standard Linux networking commands (`ip`, `nmcli`, `ping`, `dig`, `dhclient`, `systemctl`) into a single diagnostic workflow, similar to how the Windows network troubleshooter operates.

---

## Installation

**From .deb package:**

```bash
sudo dpkg -i network-recover_1.1.1_all.deb
From source:

bash
git clone https://github.com/cypso05/Linux_Network-Recovery-Tool.git
cd Linux_Network-Recovery-Tool
sudo ./install.sh
The installer places the tool at /usr/local/bin/network-recover and optionally sets up an XFCE panel launcher.

Usage
bash
sudo network-recover status      # Quick health check
sudo network-recover diagnose    # Full diagnostic (no changes made)
sudo network-recover repair      # Diagnose and attempt repairs
sudo network-recover snapshot    # Save current network state
sudo network-recover watch       # Real-time monitoring
A GUI wrapper is available at /usr/local/bin/network-recover-gui with progress dialogs and desktop notifications.

Diagnostic Layers
The tool checks connectivity in sequence, from lowest to highest level:

Physical — carrier signal, operstate, link speed

Link — interface up/down, bridge membership, vnet attachment

IP — IPv4/IPv6 address presence, duplicate IP detection

Routing — default route, blackhole routes, metrics

Gateway — ARP entry, ICMP reachability

Internet — ping to multiple public IPs (ICMP + TCP fallback)

DNS — resolv.conf parsing, resolution tests, resolver responsiveness

HTTPS — curl-based connectivity tests, captive portal detection

NetworkManager — service status, device state, stale connections

Virtualization — libvirtd status, VM count

Kubernetes — cluster connectivity (optional, skipped if not configured)

Repairs
When run with repair, the tool applies fixes based on which layers failed:

Layer Failed	Repair Action
DNS	Flush caches (resolvectl, systemd-resolve), restart systemd-resolved
Gateway or Routing	Flush ARP cache
Physical or Link	Disconnect and reconnect interface via nmcli
IP	Renew DHCP lease or restart static IP connection
NetworkManager	Restart NetworkManager service
Bridge	Bring bridge up, re-enslave detached interfaces
Repairs are applied in dependency order. DNS is not repaired before the interface is confirmed up.

Example Output
text
==========================================
    NETWORK RECOVERY REPORT
==========================================
Interface: br0
Timestamp: 2026-06-24 14:05:21

Layer Results
==========================================
PASS Physical Link
PASS Interface State
PASS IP Address
PASS Gateway Reachable
PASS External IP Reachable
FAIL DNS Resolution
FAIL HTTPS Connectivity

Root Cause: DNS Resolution Failure

Applied Repair:
  - Flushed DNS cache
  - Restarted systemd-resolved
  - Retest: DNS Resolution PASS

Final Status: CONNECTED
==========================================
Reports and Logs
Every run generates a timestamped log:

text
/var/log/network-events/
  diagnostic-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-MM-SS.log
  recovery-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-MM-SS.log
  snapshot-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-MM-SS.log
Each report includes interface state, IP addresses, routing tables, DNS configuration, gateway tests, NetworkManager status, bridge status, and recent NetworkManager journal entries.

Snapshots are saved to /var/lib/network-recover/snapshots/.

Desktop Integration (XFCE)
A panel launcher can be added next to the network icon:

Right-click the panel, select Panel > Add New Items

Search for "Network Diagnose & Repair"

Click Add

Or run the automated setup:

bash
cd Linux_Network-Recovery-Tool
./integration/xfce-integration.sh
System Requirements
Requirement	Details
Distribution	Debian, Ubuntu, MX Linux, Fedora, RHEL, Arch, and derivatives
Init system	systemd
Network management	NetworkManager
Core dependencies	bash, iproute2, curl, nmcli, ping, grep, awk
Optional	zenity (GUI), libnotify (notifications), policykit-1 (pkexec)
Known Limitations
HTTPS tests may report failures when internet is working. This can occur due to firewall rules, proxy settings, or TLS interception. If layers 1-6 pass and you can browse normally, the HTTPS test result can be disregarded.

Bridge name may display as "None" in status output on some configurations. Bridge detection and repair functions are unaffected.

Root privileges are required. The tool modifies network interfaces, restarts services, and writes to system files. Review the source before running as root.

License
MIT License. See LICENSE for details.

Commit your changes

Push to the branch

#!/bin/bash
# repairs/bridge - bridge repair action with slave re-attachment
# Usage: repair_bridge [bridge] [slave_iface]

repair_bridge() {
    local bridge="${1:-$BRIDGE_IFACE}"
    local slave="${2:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}"
    
    echo "ðŸ”§ Repairing bridge $bridge..."
    
    # Check if bridge is configured
    if [[ -z "$bridge" ]]; then
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  No bridge configured"
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Check if bridge exists
    if ! ip link show "$bridge" &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âŒ Bridge $bridge does not exist"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Show current slaves
    local current_slaves=$(bridge link show "$bridge" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '^[0-9]+: \K[^:]+' | tr '\n' ' ')
    echo "  â„¹ï¸  Current slaves: ${current_slaves:-none}"
    
    # Check if bridge is UP
    if ip link show "$bridge" | grep -q "UP"; then
        echo "  âœ… Bridge $bridge is already UP"
    else
        echo "  ðŸ“Œ Bringing bridge up..."
        # Try nmcli first
        if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null && nmcli con up "$bridge" 2>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… Bridge $bridge brought up (nmcli)"
        else
            # Try ip link as fallback
            if ip link set "$bridge" up 2>/dev/null; then
                echo "  âœ… Bridge $bridge brought up (ip link)"
            else
                echo "  âŒ Failed to bring up bridge $bridge"
                return 1
            fi
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check slave if specified
    if [[ -n "$slave" ]]; then
        # Check if slave interface exists
        if ! ip link show "$slave" &>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âš ï¸  Slave interface $slave does not exist - skipping"
            return 0
        fi
        
        # Wait briefly for state to settle
        sleep 1
        
        # Check if slave is attached to bridge
        if bridge link show "$bridge" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$slave"; then
            echo "  âœ… $slave is enslaved to $bridge"
            # Ensure slave is UP
            if ! ip link show "$slave" | grep -q "UP"; then
                echo "  âš ï¸  $slave is enslaved but DOWN - bringing up..."
                ip link set "$slave" up 2>/dev/null
                if ip link show "$slave" | grep -q "UP"; then
                    echo "  âœ… $slave is now UP"
                fi
            fi
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  $slave NOT enslaved - attempting re-attachment..."
            
            # Re-attach slave to bridge
            if ip link set "$slave" master "$bridge" 2>/dev/null; then
                echo "  âœ… $slave re-enslaved to $bridge"
                
                # Bring slave up after attachment
                ip link set "$slave" up 2>/dev/null
                sleep 1
                if ip link show "$slave" | grep -q "UP"; then
                    echo "  âœ… $slave is UP"
                else
                    echo "  âš ï¸  $slave is enslaved but DOWN"
                fi
            else
                echo "  âŒ Failed to re-enslave $slave to $bridge"
                return 1
            fi
        fi
    fi
    
    # Final verification
    sleep 1
    if ip link show "$bridge" | grep -q "UP"; then
        echo "  âœ… Bridge $bridge is operational"
        # Show final slave list
        local final_slaves=$(bridge link show "$bridge" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '^[0-9]+: \K[^:]+' | tr '\n' ' ')
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  Slaves: ${final_slaves:-none}"
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ Bridge $bridge is still DOWN"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# repairs/dhcp - DHCP repair action with static IP awareness
# Usage: repair_dhcp [iface]

repair_dhcp() {
    local iface="${1:-${BRIDGE_IFACE:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}}"
    echo "ðŸ”§ Repairing IP on $iface..."
    
    # Detect if DHCP or static
    local ip_method="static"
    if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
        if nmcli -t -f DHCP4 device show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'DHCP4.OPTION'; then
            ip_method="dhcp"
        fi
    else
        # Fallback: check if dhclient is running or if interface has DHCP lease
        if pgrep -f "dhclient.*$iface" &>/dev/null; then
            ip_method="dhcp"
        fi
    fi
    
    echo "  â„¹ï¸  IP method: $ip_method"
    
    if [[ "$ip_method" == "dhcp" ]]; then
        if ! command -v dhclient &>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âŒ dhclient not installed"
            return 1
        fi
        echo "  ðŸ“Œ Renewing DHCP lease..."
        dhclient -r "$iface" 2>/dev/null
        if timeout 10 dhclient "$iface" 2>/dev/null; then
            # Verify IP was assigned
            local new_ip=$(ip -4 addr show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '(?<=inet\s)[0-9.]+' | head -1)
            if [[ -n "$new_ip" ]]; then
                echo "  âœ… DHCP lease renewed on $iface"
                echo "  â„¹ï¸  New IP: $new_ip"
                return 0
            else
                echo "  âš ï¸  DHCP renewed but no IP assigned"
                return 1
            fi
        else
            echo "  âŒ DHCP renewal failed (timeout or error)"
            return 1
        fi
    else
        # Static IP - restart the connection via nmcli
        echo "  ðŸ“Œ Restarting static IP connection..."
        if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
            local conn_name=$(nmcli -t -f NAME,DEVICE connection show --active 2>/dev/null | grep ":${iface}$" | cut -d: -f1)
            if [[ -n "$conn_name" ]]; then
                nmcli con down "$conn_name" 2>/dev/null
                sleep 1
                if nmcli con up "$conn_name" 2>/dev/null; then
                    echo "  âœ… Static IP connection '$conn_name' restarted"
                    local current_ip=$(ip -4 addr show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '(?<=inet\s)[0-9.]+' | head -1)
                    [[ -n "$current_ip" ]] && echo "  â„¹ï¸  IP: $current_ip"
                    return 0
                else
                    echo "  âŒ Failed to restart connection '$conn_name'"
                    echo "  ðŸ“Œ Attempting interface reset..."
                    ip link set "$iface" down 2>/dev/null
                    sleep 1
                    ip link set "$iface" up 2>/dev/null
                    if ip link show "$iface" | grep -q "UP"; then
                        echo "  âœ… Interface $iface reset"
                        return 0
                    fi
                fi
            else
                echo "  âš ï¸  No active connection found for $iface"
                echo "  ðŸ“Œ Attempting interface reset..."
                ip link set "$iface" down 2>/dev/null
                sleep 1
                ip link set "$iface" up 2>/dev/null
                if ip link show "$iface" | grep -q "UP"; then
                    echo "  âœ… Interface $iface reset"
                    return 0
                fi
            fi
        else
            # No nmcli - use ip link as fallback
            echo "  ðŸ“Œ Resetting interface (ip link)..."
            ip link set "$iface" down 2>/dev/null
            sleep 1
            ip link set "$iface" up 2>/dev/null
            if ip link show "$iface" | grep -q "UP"; then
                echo "  âœ… Interface $iface reset"
                return 0
            fi
        fi
        echo "  âŒ Failed to restart static IP connection"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# repairs/dns - DNS repair action
# Usage: repair_dns

repair_dns() {
    echo "ðŸ”§ Repairing DNS..."
    local fixed=false
    
    # Flush DNS cache via resolvectl
    if command -v resolvectl &>/dev/null; then
        if resolvectl flush-caches 2>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… DNS cache flushed (resolvectl)"
            fixed=true
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  resolvectl flush failed (may not be needed)"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Flush DNS cache via systemd-resolve (legacy)
    if command -v systemd-resolve &>/dev/null; then
        if systemd-resolve --flush-caches 2>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… DNS cache flushed (systemd-resolve)"
            fixed=true
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  systemd-resolve flush failed"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Restart systemd-resolved service
    if systemctl restart systemd-resolved 2>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… systemd-resolved restarted"
        fixed=true
    else
        echo "  âš ï¸  systemd-resolved restart failed (may not be installed)"
    fi
    
    # Verify DNS is working after repair
    echo "  ðŸ” Verifying DNS resolution..."
    if nslookup google.com &>/dev/null || dig google.com +short &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… DNS resolution verified"
        fixed=true
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ DNS still failing after repair"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# repairs/interface - interface repair action
# Usage: repair_interface [iface]

repair_interface() {
    local iface="${1:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}"
    echo "ðŸ”§ Repairing interface $iface..."
    
    if ! command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âŒ nmcli not available"
        return 1
    fi
    
    nmcli device disconnect "$iface" 2>/dev/null
    sleep 1
    
    if nmcli device connect "$iface" 2>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… Interface $iface reconnected"
        sleep 2
        # Verify
        if ip link show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "UP"; then
            echo "  âœ… Interface is UP"
            return 0
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  Interface reconnected but still DOWN"
            return 1
        fi
    else
        echo "  âŒ Failed to reconnect $iface"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# repairs/nm - NetworkManager repair action
# Usage: repair_nm

repair_nm() {
    echo "ðŸ”§ Repairing NetworkManager..."
    
    # Check current status
    if systemctl is-active NetworkManager &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  NetworkManager is currently running"
    else
        echo "  âš ï¸  NetworkManager is not running"
    fi
    
    # Restart NetworkManager
    if systemctl restart NetworkManager 2>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… NetworkManager restart initiated"
        
        # Wait for service to stabilize
        echo "  â³ Waiting for NetworkManager to stabilize..."
        local max_wait=10
        local wait_count=0
        
        while [[ $wait_count -lt $max_wait ]]; do
            if systemctl is-active NetworkManager &>/dev/null; then
                echo "  âœ… NetworkManager is running"
                break
            fi
            sleep 1
            wait_count=$((wait_count + 1))
        done
        
        if [[ $wait_count -eq $max_wait ]]; then
            echo "  âš ï¸  NetworkManager did not stabilize within ${max_wait}s"
            echo "  â„¹ï¸  Check status: systemctl status NetworkManager"
            return 1
        fi
        
        # Verify NM state via nmcli
        if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
            sleep 2  # Give NM time to fully initialize
            local nm_state=$(nmcli -t -f GENERAL.STATE general status 2>/dev/null | cut -d: -f2)
            if [[ -n "$nm_state" ]]; then
                echo "  â„¹ï¸  NetworkManager state: $nm_state"
                if [[ "$nm_state" == "connected" ]] || [[ "$nm_state" == "connecting" ]]; then
                    echo "  âœ… NetworkManager is connected"
                    return 0
                else
                    echo "  âš ï¸  NetworkManager state is '$nm_state' - may need connection"
                    return 1
                fi
            fi
        fi
        
        return 0
    else
        echo "  âŒ Failed to restart NetworkManager"
        
        # Try fallback: kill and restart
        echo "  ðŸ“Œ Attempting fallback restart..."
        if pkill -f "NetworkManager" 2>/dev/null; then
            sleep 2
            if systemctl start NetworkManager 2>/dev/null; then
                echo "  âœ… NetworkManager restarted (fallback)"
                return 0
            fi
        fi
        
        echo "  âŒ All restart attempts failed"
        return 1
    fi
}

#!/bin/bash
# repairs/routing - routing repair action
# Usage: repair_routing

repair_routing() {
    echo "ðŸ”§ Repairing routing..."
    
    local gateway=$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $3; exit}' 2>/dev/null)
    
    # Always flush route cache first (gentle repair)
    echo "  ðŸ“Œ Flushing route cache..."
    if ip route flush cache 2>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… Route cache flushed"
    else
        echo "  âš ï¸  Route cache flush failed (may not be supported)"
    fi
    
    if [[ -z "$gateway" ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  No default gateway found"
        echo "  ðŸ“Œ Restarting NetworkManager to rebuild routes..."
        
        if command -v systemctl &>/dev/null; then
            if systemctl restart NetworkManager 2>/dev/null; then
                echo "  âœ… NetworkManager restarted"
                sleep 3
                
                # Verify NM is running
                if systemctl is-active NetworkManager &>/dev/null; then
                    echo "  âœ… NetworkManager is running"
                    
                    # Check if gateway appeared
                    local new_gateway=$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $3; exit}' 2>/dev/null)
                    if [[ -n "$new_gateway" ]]; then
                        echo "  âœ… Default gateway restored: $new_gateway"
                        return 0
                    else
                        echo "  âš ï¸  No default gateway after restart"
                        return 1
                    fi
                else
                    echo "  âŒ NetworkManager failed to start"
                    return 1
                fi
            else
                echo "  âŒ Failed to restart NetworkManager"
                return 1
            fi
        else
            echo "  âŒ systemctl not available - cannot restart NetworkManager"
            return 1
        fi
    else
        echo "  âœ… Default gateway: $gateway"
        
        # Verify gateway is reachable
        echo "  ðŸ“Œ Testing gateway reachability..."
        if ping -c 1 -W 2 "$gateway" &>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… Gateway $gateway reachable - routing OK"
            return 0
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  Gateway unreachable - attempting repair..."
            
            # Try ARP refresh first
            echo "  ðŸ“Œ Refreshing ARP entry for gateway..."
            if arping -c 1 -I "$DEFAULT_IFACE" "$gateway" &>/dev/null 2>&1; then
                echo "  âœ… ARP entry refreshed"
                sleep 1
                
                # Retest gateway
                if ping -c 1 -W 2 "$gateway" &>/dev/null; then
                    echo "  âœ… Gateway now reachable"
                    return 0
                fi
            fi
            
            # If still unreachable, restart NM
            echo "  ðŸ“Œ Restarting NetworkManager to rebuild routes..."
            if command -v systemctl &>/dev/null; then
                if systemctl restart NetworkManager 2>/dev/null; then
                    echo "  âœ… NetworkManager restarted"
                    sleep 3
                    
                    # Check if gateway is now reachable
                    if ping -c 1 -W 2 "$gateway" &>/dev/null; then
                        echo "  âœ… Gateway now reachable"
                        return 0
                    else
                        echo "  âŒ Gateway still unreachable after restart"
                        return 1
                    fi
                else
                    echo "  âŒ Failed to restart NetworkManager"
                    return 1
                fi
            else
                echo "  âŒ systemctl not available - cannot restart NetworkManager"
                return 1
            fi
        fi
    fi
}
