#!/bin/bash
# /usr/local/bin/network-recover
# Production-Grade Linux Network Recovery Engine
# Version: 1.1.1
# License: MIT
#
# Usage:
#   sudo network-recover diagnose   - Run full diagnostic (no changes)
#   sudo network-recover repair     - Diagnose and attempt repairs
#   sudo network-recover status     - Quick network health check
#   sudo network-recover snapshot   - Save current network state
#   sudo network-recover watch      - Monitor network health in real-time
#
# Supports: Debian, Ubuntu, Fedora, Arch, MX Linux, RHEL, etc.

set -euo pipefail

# ============================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================

readonly VERSION="1.1.1"
readonly SCRIPT_NAME="network-recover"

# Directories
readonly LOG_DIR="/var/log/network-events"
readonly SNAPSHOT_DIR="/var/lib/network-recover/snapshots"

# Test targets (multiple for redundancy)
readonly IP_TARGETS=("1.1.1.1" "8.8.8.8" "9.9.9.9")
readonly HTTPS_TARGETS=(
    "https://cloudflare.com"
    "https://google.com"
    "https://github.com"
    "https://microsoft.com"
)
readonly DNS_TARGETS=("google.com" "cloudflare.com" "github.com")
readonly CAPTIVE_PORTAL_TEST="http://connectivitycheck.gstatic.com/generate_204"

# Timeouts
readonly PING_TIMEOUT=2
readonly CURL_TIMEOUT=5
readonly DNS_TIMEOUT=3

# ============================================================
# INITIALIZATION
# ============================================================

# Timestamp must be generated at runtime, not script load time
generate_timestamp() {
    date +%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M-%S
}

mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR" "$SNAPSHOT_DIR"

# Determine the default interface dynamically with fallback
DEFAULT_IFACE=$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $5; exit}' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
if [[ -z "$DEFAULT_IFACE" ]]; then
    # No default route - find any interface with an IP address
    DEFAULT_IFACE=$(ip -4 addr show 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '^[0-9]+: \K[^:]+' | grep -v lo | head -1 || echo "eth0")
fi
DEFAULT_GATEWAY=$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $3; exit}' 2>/dev/null || echo "")

# ============================================================
# ALLOW INTERFACE OVERRIDE VIA ENVIRONMENT VARIABLE
# ============================================================
# For testing/scripting: export NETWORK_RECOVER_IFACE=br0
# This ensures the test script can specify which interface to repair
# even when the default route is missing.
if [[ -n "${NETWORK_RECOVER_IFACE:-}" ]]; then
    DEFAULT_IFACE="$NETWORK_RECOVER_IFACE"
fi

# ============================================================
# IMPROVED BRIDGE DETECTION (prioritize br0 over lxcbr0)
# ============================================================
BRIDGE_IFACE=""

# Method 1: Use ip to list bridge devices (prefer br0)
if command -v ip &>/dev/null; then
    for bridge in $(ip -br link show type bridge 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}'); do
        if [[ "$bridge" == "br0" ]]; then
            BRIDGE_IFACE="$bridge"
            break
        elif [[ -z "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]]; then
            BRIDGE_IFACE="$bridge"
        fi
    done
fi

# Method 2: Check via sysfs (fallback, prefer br0)
if [[ -z "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]]; then
    for iface in /sys/class/net/*; do
        if [[ -d "$iface/bridge" ]]; then
            local bridge_name=$(basename "$iface")
            if [[ "$bridge_name" == "br0" ]]; then
                BRIDGE_IFACE="$bridge_name"
                break
            elif [[ -z "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]]; then
                BRIDGE_IFACE="$bridge_name"
            fi
        fi
    done
fi

# Method 3: Check via NetworkManager (last resort)
if [[ -z "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]] && command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
    BRIDGE_IFACE=$(nmcli -t -f TYPE,DEVICE connection show --active 2>/dev/null | \
        grep "^bridge:" | cut -d: -f2 | grep -v "^lxc" | head -1)
fi

# If still empty, fallback to DEFAULT_IFACE
if [[ -z "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]]; then
    BRIDGE_IFACE="$DEFAULT_IFACE"
fi

# Detect virtualization
HAS_LIBVIRT=$(command -v virsh &>/dev/null && echo "true" || echo "false")
HAS_KUBECTL=$(command -v kubectl &>/dev/null && echo "true" || echo "false")
HAS_DOCKER=$(command -v docker &>/dev/null && echo "true" || echo "false")

# ============================================================
# SOURCE MODULAR COMPONENTS (if available)
# ============================================================
# These override the built-in functions with modular versions
# when the tool is installed with full modular support.
# Falls back gracefully to built-in implementations if modules
# are not present (standalone/core-only installation).
readonly MODULE_DIR="/usr/local/lib/network-recover"

if [[ -d "$MODULE_DIR/diagnostics" ]]; then
    for mod in "$MODULE_DIR/diagnostics"/*; do
        [[ -f "$mod" ]] && source "$mod" 2>/dev/null
    done
fi

if [[ -d "$MODULE_DIR/repairs" ]]; then
    for mod in "$MODULE_DIR/repairs"/*; do
        [[ -f "$mod" ]] && source "$mod" 2>/dev/null
    done
fi

if [[ -d "$MODULE_DIR/collectors" ]]; then
    for mod in "$MODULE_DIR/collectors"/*; do
        [[ -f "$mod" ]] && source "$mod" 2>/dev/null
    done
fi

# ============================================================
# HELPERS
# ============================================================

log() {
    local level="${1:-INFO}"
    shift
    echo "[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] [$level] $*"
}

log_result() {
    local status="$1"
    local message="$2"
    if [[ "$status" == "PASS" ]]; then
        echo -e "  âœ… $message"
    elif [[ "$status" == "FAIL" ]]; then
        echo -e "  âŒ $message"
    elif [[ "$status" == "WARN" ]]; then
        echo -e "  âš ï¸  $message"
    elif [[ "$status" == "INFO" ]]; then
        echo -e "  â„¹ï¸  $message"
    else
        echo "  $message"
    fi
}

save_snapshot() {
    local TIMESTAMP=$(generate_timestamp)
    local snapshot_file="$SNAPSHOT_DIR/snapshot-${TIMESTAMP}.log"
    {
        echo "=== NETWORK SNAPSHOT: $TIMESTAMP ==="
        echo "=== System Info ==="
        uname -a
        cat /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null || echo "No os-release"
        echo ""
        echo "=== Interface Info ==="
        ip addr show 2>/dev/null || echo "No interfaces"
        echo ""
        echo "=== Route Table ==="
        ip route show 2>/dev/null || echo "No routes"
        echo ""
        echo "=== ARP Table ==="
        ip neigh show 2>/dev/null || echo "No ARP"
        echo ""
        echo "=== Bridge Info ==="
        bridge link show 2>/dev/null || echo "No bridges found"
        echo ""
        echo "=== NetworkManager Status ==="
        nmcli general status 2>/dev/null || echo "NetworkManager not available"
        nmcli device status 2>/dev/null || echo ""
        echo ""
        echo "=== DNS Configuration ==="
        cat /etc/resolv.conf 2>/dev/null || echo "No resolv.conf"
        resolvectl status 2>/dev/null || echo "resolvectl not available"
        echo ""
        echo "=== Active Connections ==="
        ss -tulpn 2>/dev/null | head -50
        echo ""
        echo "=== Recent Network Logs ==="
        journalctl -u NetworkManager -n 50 --no-pager 2>/dev/null || echo "No NetworkManager logs"
        echo ""
        echo "=== Kernel Network Stats ==="
        cat /proc/net/dev 2>/dev/null || echo "No stats"
        echo ""
        echo "=== End Snapshot ==="
    } > "$snapshot_file"
    echo "$snapshot_file"
}

# ============================================================
# UTILITY: Detect IP assignment method
# ============================================================

detect_ip_method() {
    local iface="$1"
    if [[ -z "$iface" ]]; then
        echo "unknown"
        return
    fi
    # Check if DHCP is configured via NetworkManager
    if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
        if nmcli -t -f DHCP4 device show "$iface" 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'DHCP4.OPTION'; then
            echo "dhcp"
            return
        fi
    fi
    # Check for dhclient lease files
    if [[ -f "/var/lib/dhcp/dhclient.${iface}.leases" ]] || [[ -f "/var/lib/dhclient/dhclient-${iface}.leases" ]]; then
        echo "dhcp"
        return
    fi
    # Default to static
    echo "static"
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 1: PHYSICAL LAYER
# ============================================================

check_physical() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 1: PHYSICAL ==="
    local all_pass=true
    
    if [[ -z "$DEFAULT_IFACE" ]]; then
        log_result "FAIL" "No interface detected"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Check if interface exists
    if [[ ! -d "/sys/class/net/$DEFAULT_IFACE" ]]; then
        log_result "FAIL" "Interface $DEFAULT_IFACE does not exist"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Check carrier - with bridge interface support
    if [[ -f "/sys/class/net/$DEFAULT_IFACE/carrier" ]] && [[ -r "/sys/class/net/$DEFAULT_IFACE/carrier" ]]; then
        local carrier=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$DEFAULT_IFACE/carrier" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
        if [[ "$carrier" == "1" ]]; then
            log_result "PASS" "Carrier: present"
        elif [[ "$carrier" != "0" ]]; then
            # Bridge or virtual interface without carrier support
            log_result "INFO" "Carrier: not applicable for this interface type"
        else
            log_result "FAIL" "Carrier: LOST - Check cable/WiFi"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    else
        # Bridge interfaces often have carrier file but can't be read
        # Check if it's a bridge interface
        if ip link show "$DEFAULT_IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "bridge"; then
            log_result "INFO" "Carrier: not applicable for bridge interface"
        else
            log_result "INFO" "Carrier: not available for this interface"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check operstate
    if [[ -f "/sys/class/net/$DEFAULT_IFACE/operstate" ]]; then
        local state=$(cat "/sys/class/net/$DEFAULT_IFACE/operstate" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")
        if [[ "$state" == "up" ]]; then
            log_result "PASS" "Interface state: $state"
        else
            log_result "FAIL" "Interface state: $state (should be up)"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check speed and duplex (ethernet only) - with timeout to prevent hangs
    if command -v ethtool &>/dev/null && [[ -e "/sys/class/net/$DEFAULT_IFACE/device" ]]; then
        local speed=$(timeout 2 ethtool "$DEFAULT_IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -i "Speed:" | awk '{print $2}')
        local duplex=$(timeout 2 ethtool "$DEFAULT_IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -i "Duplex:" | awk '{print $2}')
        if [[ -n "$speed" ]]; then
            log_result "INFO" "Speed: $speed, Duplex: $duplex"
        fi
    fi
    
    if [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]]; then
        return 0
    else
        return 1
    fi
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 2: LINK LAYER (Bridge, Bond, VLAN)
# ============================================================

check_link_layer() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 2: LINK LAYER ==="
    local all_pass=true
    
    if [[ -z "$DEFAULT_IFACE" ]]; then
        log_result "FAIL" "No interface detected"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Check if interface is UP
    if ip link show "$DEFAULT_IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "UP"; then
        log_result "PASS" "Interface $DEFAULT_IFACE is UP"
    else
        log_result "FAIL" "Interface $DEFAULT_IFACE is DOWN"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check bridge if present
    if [[ -n "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]]; then
        if ip link show "$BRIDGE_IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "UP"; then
            log_result "PASS" "Bridge $BRIDGE_IFACE is UP"
        else
            log_result "FAIL" "Bridge $BRIDGE_IFACE is DOWN"
            all_pass=false
        fi
        
        # Check if interface is enslaved to bridge
        if bridge link show 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$DEFAULT_IFACE"; then
            log_result "PASS" "$DEFAULT_IFACE enslaved to $BRIDGE_IFACE"
        else
            log_result "WARN" "$DEFAULT_IFACE NOT enslaved to $BRIDGE_IFACE"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check for vnet interfaces (KVM)
    if [[ "$HAS_LIBVIRT" == "true" ]]; then
        local vnets=$(ip link show 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'vnet\d+' | sort -u)
        if [[ -n "$vnets" ]]; then
            for vnet in $vnets; do
                if bridge link show 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$vnet"; then
                    log_result "PASS" "$vnet is bridged"
                else
                    log_result "WARN" "$vnet NOT bridged"
                    all_pass=false
                fi
            done
        fi
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 3: IP LAYER
# ============================================================

check_ip_layer() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 3: IP ==="
    local all_pass=true
    
    local iface_to_check="${BRIDGE_IFACE:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}"
    if [[ -z "$iface_to_check" ]]; then
        log_result "FAIL" "No interface to check"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Check IPv4 address - FIXED: using \K instead of lookbehind
    local ipv4=$(ip -4 addr show "$iface_to_check" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'inet\s\K\d+(\.\d+){3}/\d+' | head -1)
    
    if [[ -n "$ipv4" ]]; then
        log_result "PASS" "IPv4: $ipv4"
        
        # Check for duplicate IP - FIXED: compare MAC addresses
        local ip_only=$(echo "$ipv4" | cut -d/ -f1)
        local my_mac=$(ip link show "$iface_to_check" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'link/ether \K[^ ]+')
        if [[ -n "$my_mac" ]]; then
            local arp_output=$(arping -c 2 -I "$iface_to_check" "$ip_only" 2>/dev/null)
            local arp_mac=$(echo "$arp_output" | grep -oP 'from \K[^ ]+' | head -1)
            if [[ -n "$arp_mac" ]] && [[ "$arp_mac" != "$my_mac" ]]; then
                log_result "FAIL" "Duplicate IP detected! Own MAC: $my_mac, Duplicate MAC: $arp_mac"
                all_pass=false
            fi
        fi
    else
        log_result "FAIL" "No IPv4 address on $iface_to_check"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check IPv6 - FIXED: using \K instead of lookbehind
    local ipv6=$(ip -6 addr show "$iface_to_check" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'inet6\s\K[a-f0-9:]+' | grep -v "^::1" | head -1)
    if [[ -n "$ipv6" ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "IPv6: $ipv6"
    else
        log_result "INFO" "No IPv6 address (may be disabled)"
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 4: ROUTING
# ============================================================

check_routing() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 4: ROUTING ==="
    local all_pass=true
    
    # Check default route
    if [[ -n "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" ]]; then
        log_result "PASS" "Default gateway: $DEFAULT_GATEWAY"
    else
        log_result "FAIL" "No default route found"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Check for multiple default routes (common problem)
    local default_count=$(ip route 2>/dev/null | grep -c "^default" || echo "0")
    if [[ "$default_count" -gt 1 ]]; then
        log_result "WARN" "Multiple default routes found ($default_count)"
        ip route | grep "^default"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check for blackhole routes
    if ip route 2>/dev/null | grep -q "blackhole"; then
        log_result "WARN" "Blackhole routes detected"
        ip route | grep "blackhole"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check route metrics
    if ip route 2>/dev/null | grep -q "metric"; then
        log_result "INFO" "Route metrics configured"
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 5: GATEWAY
# ============================================================

check_gateway() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 5: GATEWAY ==="
    
    if [[ -z "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" ]]; then
        log_result "FAIL" "No default gateway - skipping gateway checks"
        return 1
    fi
    
    local all_pass=true
    
    # Check ARP entry
    if ip neigh show "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "REACHABLE"; then
        log_result "PASS" "Gateway ARP: REACHABLE"
    elif ip neigh show "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "STALE"; then
        log_result "WARN" "Gateway ARP: STALE - may need refresh"
        all_pass=false
    else
        log_result "WARN" "No ARP entry for gateway"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Ping gateway with short timeout
    if ping -c 1 -W "$PING_TIMEOUT" "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" &>/dev/null; then
        log_result "PASS" "Gateway reachable (ICMP)"
    else
        # Try ARP ping
        if arping -c 1 -W 1 "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" &>/dev/null; then
            log_result "WARN" "Gateway responds to ARP but not ICMP"
        else
            log_result "FAIL" "Gateway unreachable"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 6: INTERNET REACHABILITY
# ============================================================

check_internet() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 6: INTERNET REACHABILITY ==="
    local reachable_count=0
    local total_targets=${#IP_TARGETS[@]}
    
    for target in "${IP_TARGETS[@]}"; do
        if ping -c 1 -W "$PING_TIMEOUT" "$target" &>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "$target reachable (ICMP)"
            ((reachable_count++))
        else
            # Try TCP connect as fallback
            if command -v nc &>/dev/null && nc -zv -w 3 "$target" 443 &>/dev/null 2>&1; then
                log_result "PASS" "$target reachable (TCP/443)"
                ((reachable_count++))
            else
                log_result "WARN" "$target unreachable"
            fi
        fi
    done
    
    # Require quorum (at least 2 out of 3)
    if [[ "$reachable_count" -ge 2 ]]; then
        log_result "PASS" "Internet reachable ($reachable_count/$total_targets)"
        return 0
    else
        log_result "FAIL" "Internet unreachable ($reachable_count/$total_targets)"
        return 1
    fi
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 7: DNS
# ============================================================

check_dns() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 7: DNS ==="
    local all_pass=true
    
    # Check resolv.conf
    if [[ -f /etc/resolv.conf ]]; then
        local nameservers=$(grep "^nameserver" /etc/resolv.conf 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        if [[ "$nameservers" -gt 0 ]]; then
            log_result "PASS" "DNS servers configured ($nameservers)"
            grep "^nameserver" /etc/resolv.conf 2>/dev/null | while read -r line; do
                log_result "INFO" "  $line"
            done
        else
            log_result "FAIL" "No nameservers in /etc/resolv.conf"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    fi
    
    # Test DNS resolution with multiple servers
    local dns_success=0
    for domain in "${DNS_TARGETS[@]}"; do
        if nslookup "$domain" &>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "$domain resolves"
            ((dns_success++))
        else
            # Try with specific DNS server
            if dig "@1.1.1.1" "$domain" +timeout=2 &>/dev/null; then
                log_result "PASS" "$domain resolves (via 1.1.1.1)"
                ((dns_success++))
            else
                log_result "WARN" "$domain resolution failed"
            fi
        fi
    done
    
    # Check for DNS anomalies (meaningful security checks)
    if command -v dig &>/dev/null; then
        local dns_anomaly=false
        local test_domain="google.com"  # Using a stable, well-known domain
        
        # Test 1: Detect hijacking via private IP responses
        # If google.com resolves to a private/loopback IP, DNS is hijacked
        local hijack_test=$(dig +short "$test_domain" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
        if [[ -n "$hijack_test" ]]; then
            # Check for private, loopback, or link-local addresses
            if [[ "$hijack_test" =~ ^(127\.|10\.|192\.168\.|172\.1[6-9]\.|172\.2[0-9]\.|172\.3[0-1]\.|0\.|169\.254\.) ]]; then
                log_result "FAIL" "DNS hijacking detected - $test_domain resolves to private IP: $hijack_test"
                dns_anomaly=true
                all_pass=false
            fi
        fi
        
        # Test 2: Detect DNS interception via response time anomaly
        # If the first query takes > 500ms, something is intercepting DNS
        local dns_time=$(dig "$test_domain" +time=1 +tries=1 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'Query time: \K[0-9]+' || echo "0")
        if [[ "$dns_time" -gt 500 ]] && [[ "$dns_time" != "0" ]]; then
            log_result "WARN" "DNS response time high (${dns_time}ms) - possible interception"
            dns_anomaly=true
        fi
        
        # Test 3: Verify configured DNS servers actually respond
        local ns_list=$(grep "^nameserver" /etc/resolv.conf 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $2}')
        local dead_ns=0
        for ns in $ns_list; do
            if ! dig @"$ns" "$test_domain" +time=1 +tries=1 &>/dev/null; then
                log_result "WARN" "DNS server $ns not responding"
                dns_anomaly=true
                ((dead_ns++))
            fi
        done
        
        # Test 4: Response consistency (optional - detects cache poisoning)
        local ip1=$(dig +short "$test_domain" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
        sleep 0.5
        local ip2=$(dig +short "$test_domain" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
        if [[ -n "$ip1" ]] && [[ -n "$ip2" ]] && [[ "$ip1" != "$ip2" ]] && [[ ! "$ip1" =~ ^(127\.|10\.|192\.168\.) ]] && [[ ! "$ip2" =~ ^(127\.|10\.|192\.168\.) ]]; then
            log_result "INFO" "DNS responses vary (normal load balancing)"
            log_result "INFO" "  $test_domain: $ip1 and $ip2"
        fi
        
        if [[ "$dns_anomaly" == "false" ]] && [[ -n "$ns_list" ]]; then
            log_result "PASS" "DNS integrity check passed"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check systemd-resolved status
    if command -v resolvectl &>/dev/null; then
        local dns_state=$(resolvectl status 2>/dev/null | grep -i "DNS Servers" | head -1)
        if [[ -n "$dns_state" ]]; then
            log_result "INFO" "systemd-resolved: $dns_state"
        fi
    fi
    
    if [[ "$dns_success" -ge 2 ]]; then
        return 0
    else
        return 1
    fi
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 8: HTTPS / TLS / ACTUAL BROWSING
# ============================================================

check_https() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 8: HTTPS & BROWSING ==="
    local all_pass=true
    local https_success=0
    
    if ! command -v curl &>/dev/null; then
        log_result "WARN" "curl not installed - skipping HTTPS tests"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Test captive portal detection
    # HTTP 000 means connection failed, not a captive portal
    # Only flag as captive portal when we get an actual HTTP redirect (2xx/3xx)
    local portal_response=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" --connect-timeout 5 "$CAPTIVE_PORTAL_TEST" 2>/dev/null)
    if [[ "$portal_response" == "204" ]]; then
        log_result "PASS" "No captive portal detected"
    elif [[ "$portal_response" == "000" ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "Captive portal test: connection failed (no internet)"
    elif [[ "$portal_response" =~ ^[23][0-9][0-9]$ ]]; then
        log_result "WARN" "Captive portal detected (HTTP $portal_response)"
        log_result "INFO" "You may need to log in to the network"
        all_pass=false
    else
        log_result "WARN" "Captive portal test: unexpected response (HTTP $portal_response)"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Test HTTPS targets
    for target in "${HTTPS_TARGETS[@]}"; do
        local http_code=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" --connect-timeout "$CURL_TIMEOUT" --max-time 10 "$target" 2>/dev/null)
        if [[ "$http_code" =~ ^[23][0-9][0-9]$ ]]; then
            log_result "PASS" "HTTPS: $target (HTTP $http_code)"
            ((https_success++))
        elif [[ "$http_code" == "000" ]]; then
            log_result "WARN" "HTTPS: $target (connection failed)"
            all_pass=false
        else
            log_result "WARN" "HTTPS: $target (HTTP $http_code)"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    done
    
    # Skip MTU test if there's no internet (all HTTPS failed with 000)
    if [[ "$https_success" -eq 0 ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "Skipping MTU test - no internet connectivity"
    elif [[ "$https_success" -lt 2 ]] && [[ -n "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "Testing MTU..."
        if ping -M do -s 1472 -c 1 "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" &>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "MTU: 1500 works"
        else
            log_result "WARN" "MTU issues detected - try reducing MTU to 1400"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 9: NETWORKMANAGER
# ============================================================

check_networkmanager() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 9: NETWORKMANAGER ==="
    local all_pass=true
    
    if ! command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
        log_result "INFO" "NetworkManager not installed (using other network stack)"
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Check if NM is running
    if systemctl is-active NetworkManager &>/dev/null; then
        log_result "PASS" "NetworkManager is running"
    else
        log_result "FAIL" "NetworkManager is NOT running"
        return 1
    fi
    
    # Check NM state
    local nm_state=$(nmcli -t -f GENERAL.STATE general status 2>/dev/null | cut -d: -f2)
    if [[ -n "$nm_state" ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "NetworkManager state: $nm_state"
        if [[ "$nm_state" == "connected" ]] || [[ "$nm_state" == "connecting" ]]; then
            log_result "PASS" "NetworkManager has connection"
        else
            log_result "WARN" "NetworkManager state is '$nm_state'"
            all_pass=false
        fi
    fi
    
    # Check device status (using process substitution to avoid subshell)
    local connected_count=0
    while IFS= read -r line; do
        if [[ "$line" =~ connected ]]; then
            log_result "PASS" "$line"
            ((connected_count++)) || true
        fi
    done < <(nmcli device status 2>/dev/null)
    
    # Check for stale connections - ROBUST VERSION
    local stale_connections=0
    local nm_output
    nm_output=$(nmcli connection show --active 2>/dev/null || true)
    if [[ -n "$nm_output" ]]; then
        stale_connections=$(echo "$nm_output" | grep -c "stale" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
        # Ensure it's a clean number
        stale_connections=$(echo "$stale_connections" | tr -d '[:space:]')
        # Default to 0 if not a number
        if ! [[ "$stale_connections" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
            stale_connections=0
        fi
    fi
    
    if [[ "$stale_connections" -gt 0 ]]; then
        log_result "WARN" "Stale connections detected ($stale_connections)"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 10: VIRTUALIZATION (KVM/Libvirt)
# ============================================================

check_virtualization() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 10: VIRTUALIZATION ==="
    
    if [[ "$HAS_LIBVIRT" != "true" ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "No libvirt detected - skipping virtualization checks"
        return 0
    fi
    
    local all_pass=true
    
    # Check libvirtd
    if systemctl is-active libvirtd &>/dev/null; then
        log_result "PASS" "libvirtd is running"
    else
        log_result "WARN" "libvirtd is NOT running"
        all_pass=false
    fi
    
    # Check VMs
    if command -v virsh &>/dev/null; then
        local running_vms=$(virsh list --state-running --name 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        local defined_vms=$(virsh list --all --name 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        log_result "INFO" "VMs: $running_vms running / $defined_vms defined"
    fi
    
    [[ "$all_pass" == "true" ]] && return 0 || return 1
}

# ============================================================
# LAYER 11: KUBERNETES (Optional)
# ============================================================

check_kubernetes() {
    log "INFO" "=== LAYER 11: KUBERNETES ==="
    
    if [[ "$HAS_KUBECTL" != "true" ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "No kubectl detected - skipping Kubernetes checks"
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Check if kubeconfig exists
    local kubeconfig="${KUBECONFIG:-$HOME/.kube/config}"
    if [[ ! -f "$kubeconfig" ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "No kubeconfig found - skipping Kubernetes checks"
        return 0
    fi
    
    # Test cluster connectivity
    if kubectl cluster-info --request-timeout=5s &>/dev/null; then
        log_result "PASS" "Kubernetes API accessible"
        
        local nodes=$(kubectl get nodes --no-headers 2>/dev/null | grep -c " Ready" || echo "0")
        local total=$(kubectl get nodes --no-headers 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo "0")
        log_result "INFO" "Kubernetes: $nodes/$total nodes ready"
    else
        log_result "WARN" "Kubernetes API not accessible"
        return 1
    fi
    
    return 0
}

# ============================================================
# REPAIR ENGINE - Layer-aware, static IP aware
# ============================================================

repair_network() {
    log "INFO" "=== REPAIR ENGINE ==="
    log "INFO" "Starting targeted repairs based on diagnostic results..."
    local repair_success_count=0
    local repair_attempt_count=0
    local iface_to_repair="${BRIDGE_IFACE:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}"
    
    if [[ -z "$iface_to_repair" ]]; then
        log_result "WARN" "No interface to repair - attempting to find one"
        iface_to_repair=$(ip -br link show 2>/dev/null | grep -v lo | awk '{print $1}' | head -1)
        if [[ -z "$iface_to_repair" ]]; then
            log_result "FAIL" "No network interface found"
            return 1
        fi
    fi
    
    local ip_method=$(detect_ip_method "$iface_to_repair")
    
    log "INFO" "IP method detected: $ip_method on $iface_to_repair"
    
    # Stage 1: DNS repairs (only if DNS failed)
    if [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[dns]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 1: DNS repairs (DNS layer failed)"
        ((repair_attempt_count++))
        local dns_fixed=false
        
        if command -v resolvectl &>/dev/null; then
            if resolvectl flush-caches 2>/dev/null; then
                log_result "PASS" "DNS cache flushed (resolvectl)"
                dns_fixed=true
            fi
        fi
        if command -v systemd-resolve &>/dev/null; then
            if systemd-resolve --flush-caches 2>/dev/null; then
                log_result "PASS" "DNS cache flushed (systemd-resolve)"
                dns_fixed=true
            fi
        fi
        if command -v nscd &>/dev/null; then
            if systemctl restart nscd 2>/dev/null; then
                log_result "PASS" "nscd restarted"
                dns_fixed=true
            fi
        fi
        if systemctl restart systemd-resolved 2>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "systemd-resolved restarted"
            dns_fixed=true
        fi
        
        if [[ "$dns_fixed" == "true" ]]; then
            ((repair_success_count++))
        else
            log_result "WARN" "DNS repairs had no effect"
        fi
    else
        log "INFO" "Stage 1: DNS OK - skipping DNS repairs"
    fi
    
    # Stage 2: ARP cache (only if gateway or routing failed)
    if [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[gateway]:-0} -ne 0 ]] || [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[routing]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 2: Flushing ARP cache (gateway/routing layer failed)"
        ((repair_attempt_count++))
        if ip neigh flush all 2>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "ARP cache flushed"
            ((repair_success_count++))
        else
            log_result "WARN" "Failed to flush ARP cache"
        fi
    else
        log "INFO" "Stage 2: Gateway/Routing OK - skipping ARP flush"
    fi
    
    # Stage 3: Interface reconnect (if physical or link layer failed)
    if [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[physical]:-0} -ne 0 ]] || [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[link]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 3: Reconnecting interface (physical/link layer failed)"
        ((repair_attempt_count++))
        if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
            if nmcli device status 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$iface_to_repair"; then
                nmcli device disconnect "$iface_to_repair" 2>/dev/null || true
                sleep 1
                if nmcli device connect "$iface_to_repair" 2>/dev/null; then
                    log_result "PASS" "Interface $iface_to_repair reconnected"
                    ((repair_success_count++))
                else
                    log_result "WARN" "Failed to reconnect $iface_to_repair"
                fi
                sleep 2
            fi
        fi
    else
        log "INFO" "Stage 3: Physical/Link OK - skipping interface reconnect"
    fi
    
    # Stage 4: IP/DHCP repair (only if IP layer failed)
    if [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[ip]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 4: IP repair (IP layer failed)"
        ((repair_attempt_count++))
        
        if [[ "$ip_method" == "dhcp" ]]; then
            log "INFO" "Renewing DHCP lease on $iface_to_repair"
            if command -v dhclient &>/dev/null; then
                dhclient -r "$iface_to_repair" 2>/dev/null || true
                if dhclient "$iface_to_repair" 2>/dev/null; then
                    log_result "PASS" "DHCP lease renewed on $iface_to_repair"
                    ((repair_success_count++))
                else
                    log_result "WARN" "Failed to renew DHCP lease"
                fi
            fi
        else
            log "INFO" "Static IP detected - restarting connection instead"
            if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
                local conn_name=$(nmcli -t -f NAME,DEVICE connection show --active 2>/dev/null | grep ":${iface_to_repair}$" | cut -d: -f1)
                if [[ -n "$conn_name" ]]; then
                    nmcli con down "$conn_name" 2>/dev/null || true
                    sleep 1
                    if nmcli con up "$conn_name" 2>/dev/null; then
                        log_result "PASS" "Static IP connection '$conn_name' restarted"
                        ((repair_success_count++))
                    else
                        log_result "WARN" "Failed to restart connection '$conn_name'"
                    fi
                else
                    log_result "WARN" "Could not find connection name for $iface_to_repair"
                fi
            fi
        fi
    else
        log "INFO" "Stage 4: IP layer OK - skipping IP repair"
    fi
    
    # Stage 5: NetworkManager restart (if NM layer failed)
    if [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[nm]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 5: Restarting NetworkManager (NM layer failed)"
        ((repair_attempt_count++))
        if systemctl restart NetworkManager 2>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "NetworkManager restarted"
            ((repair_success_count++))
            sleep 3
        else
            log_result "WARN" "Failed to restart NetworkManager"
        fi
    else
        log "INFO" "Stage 5: NetworkManager OK - skipping NM restart"
    fi
    
    # Stage 6: Resolver restart (if DNS failed and not already done)
    if [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[dns]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 6: Restarting resolver (DNS layer failed)"
        ((repair_attempt_count++))
        if systemctl restart systemd-resolved 2>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "systemd-resolved restarted"
            ((repair_success_count++))
        fi
    fi
    
    # Stage 7: Bridge recovery (if bridge issues detected)
    if [[ -n "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]] && [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[link]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 7: Bridge recovery (link layer failed)"
        ((repair_attempt_count++))
        if nmcli con up "$BRIDGE_IFACE" 2>/dev/null; then
            log_result "PASS" "Bridge $BRIDGE_IFACE brought up"
            ((repair_success_count++))
            
            # Re-enslave interface if needed
            if ! bridge link show 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$iface_to_repair"; then
                log "INFO" "Re-enslaving $iface_to_repair to $BRIDGE_IFACE"
                if ip link set "$iface_to_repair" master "$BRIDGE_IFACE" 2>/dev/null; then
                    log_result "PASS" "$iface_to_repair re-enslaved to $BRIDGE_IFACE"
                else
                    log_result "WARN" "Failed to re-enslave $iface_to_repair"
                fi
            fi
        else
            log_result "WARN" "Failed to bring up bridge $BRIDGE_IFACE"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Stage 8: DNS fallback (last resort, only if DNS still broken)
    if [[ ${LAYER_STATUS[dns]:-0} -ne 0 ]]; then
        log "INFO" "Stage 8: DNS fallback configuration"
        if [[ ! -f /etc/resolv.conf ]]; then
            echo "nameserver 1.1.1.1" > /etc/resolv.conf
            echo "nameserver 8.8.8.8" >> /etc/resolv.conf
            log_result "PASS" "Fallback DNS configured (new resolv.conf)"
            ((repair_success_count++))
            ((repair_attempt_count++))
        elif [[ -L /etc/resolv.conf ]]; then
            log_result "INFO" "resolv.conf is a symlink (managed by resolver) - not modifying"
        elif ! grep -q "^nameserver" /etc/resolv.conf; then
            echo "nameserver 1.1.1.1" >> /etc/resolv.conf
            echo "nameserver 8.8.8.8" >> /etc/resolv.conf
            log_result "PASS" "Fallback DNS added to resolv.conf"
            ((repair_success_count++))
            ((repair_attempt_count++))
        else
            log_result "INFO" "resolv.conf already has nameservers - skipping"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Report results
    if [[ "$repair_success_count" -gt 0 ]]; then
        log_result "PASS" "Repairs completed: $repair_success_count/$repair_attempt_count succeeded"
        return 0
    elif [[ "$repair_attempt_count" -eq 0 ]]; then
        log_result "INFO" "No repairs needed - all layers healthy"
        return 0
    else
        log_result "FAIL" "No repairs succeeded (0/$repair_attempt_count)"
        return 1
    fi
}

# ============================================================
# REPORT GENERATION
# ============================================================

generate_report() {
    local results_file="$1"
    echo ""
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "    NETWORK RECOVERY REPORT"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "Time: $(date)"
    echo "Host: $(hostname)"
    echo "Interface: $DEFAULT_IFACE"
    [[ -n "$BRIDGE_IFACE" ]] && echo "Bridge: $BRIDGE_IFACE"
    [[ -n "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" ]] && echo "Gateway: $DEFAULT_GATEWAY"
    echo "------------------------------------------"
    cat "$results_file"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "Full report saved to: $results_file"
    echo "Snapshots saved to: $SNAPSHOT_DIR"
    echo "=========================================="
}

# ============================================================
# STATUS COMMAND (Quick health check)
# ============================================================

cmd_status() {
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "    NETWORK STATUS"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "Time: $(date)"
    echo "Host: $(hostname)"
    echo "------------------------------------------"
    echo "Interface: $DEFAULT_IFACE"
    
    # Fix IP detection - use bridge if available, fallback to default
    local ip_addr=$(ip -4 addr show "${BRIDGE_IFACE}" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'inet\s+\K[0-9.]+' | head -1)
    if [[ -z "$ip_addr" ]]; then
        ip_addr=$(ip -4 addr show "${DEFAULT_IFACE}" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'inet\s+\K[0-9.]+' | head -1 || echo "None")
    fi
    echo "IP: $ip_addr"
    
    echo "Gateway: ${DEFAULT_GATEWAY:-'None'}"
    echo "Bridge: ${BRIDGE_IFACE:-None}"
    echo "------------------------------------------"
    
    if ping -c 1 -W 1 8.8.8.8 &>/dev/null; then
        echo "Internet: âœ… Connected"
    else
        echo "Internet: âŒ Unreachable"
    fi
    
    if nslookup google.com &>/dev/null 2>&1; then
        echo "DNS:      âœ… Working"
    else
        echo "DNS:      âŒ Failed"
    fi
    
    if systemctl is-active NetworkManager &>/dev/null; then
        local nm_state=$(nmcli -t -f GENERAL.STATE general status 2>/dev/null | cut -d: -f2)
        echo "NM:       âœ… Running (${nm_state:-unknown})"
    else
        echo "NM:       âŒ Stopped"
    fi
    
    if [[ "$HAS_LIBVIRT" == "true" ]]; then
        if systemctl is-active libvirtd &>/dev/null; then
            local vm_count=$(virsh list --state-running --name 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
            echo "VMs:      âœ… $vm_count running"
        else
            echo "VMs:      âŒ libvirtd stopped"
        fi
    fi
    
    echo "=========================================="
}

# ============================================================
# MAIN COMMANDS
# ============================================================

cmd_diagnose() {
    log "INFO" "=== NETWORK DIAGNOSTIC STARTED ==="
    local TIMESTAMP=$(generate_timestamp)
    local results_file="$LOG_DIR/diagnostic-${TIMESTAMP}.log"
    {
        check_physical 2>&1 || true
        check_link_layer 2>&1 || true
        check_ip_layer 2>&1 || true
        check_routing 2>&1 || true
        check_gateway 2>&1 || true
        check_internet 2>&1 || true
        check_dns 2>&1 || true
        check_https 2>&1 || true
        check_networkmanager 2>&1 || true
        check_virtualization 2>&1 || true
        check_kubernetes 2>&1 || true
    } | tee -a "$results_file"
    generate_report "$results_file"
}

cmd_repair() {
    log "INFO" "=== NETWORK RECOVERY STARTED ==="
    local TIMESTAMP=$(generate_timestamp)

    local snapshot_file=$(save_snapshot)
    log "INFO" "Pre-repair snapshot: $snapshot_file"

    local results_file="$LOG_DIR/recovery-${TIMESTAMP}.log"
    local diag_output
    diag_output=$(mktemp)
    trap "rm -f '$diag_output'" EXIT

    declare -gA LAYER_STATUS

    # Run each check individually to avoid subshell issues
    run_check() {
        local layer="$1"
        shift
        local tmp_file=$(mktemp)
        "$@" > "$tmp_file" 2>&1
        local result=$?
        LAYER_STATUS["$layer"]=$result
        cat "$tmp_file" | tee -a "$diag_output"
        rm -f "$tmp_file"
        return $result
    }

    run_check physical check_physical || true
    run_check link check_link_layer || true
    run_check ip check_ip_layer || true
    run_check routing check_routing || true
    run_check gateway check_gateway || true
    run_check internet check_internet || true
    run_check dns check_dns || true
    run_check https check_https || true
    run_check nm check_networkmanager || true
    run_check virt check_virtualization || true
    run_check k8s check_kubernetes || true

    echo ""
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "    ATTEMPTING REPAIRS"
    echo "=========================================="

    repair_network
    local repair_result=$?

    echo ""
    echo "=========================================="
    echo "    VERIFYING REPAIRS"
    echo "=========================================="
    sleep 3

    echo -e "\n=== POST-REPAIR VERIFICATION ==="
    check_internet || true
    check_dns || true
    check_https || true

    cat "$diag_output" | tee -a "$results_file"
    generate_report "$results_file"

    return $repair_result
}

cmd_snapshot() {
    local snapshot_file=$(save_snapshot)
    echo "âœ… Snapshot saved to: $snapshot_file"
    echo ""
    echo "Quick summary:"
    echo "  Interface: $DEFAULT_IFACE"
    echo "  Gateway: ${DEFAULT_GATEWAY:-'None'}"
    echo "  Bridge: ${BRIDGE_IFACE:-'None'}"
    echo "  IP: $(ip -4 addr show "${BRIDGE_IFACE:-$DEFAULT_IFACE}" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'inet\s\K\d+(\.\d+){3}' | head -1 || echo 'None')"
}

cmd_watch() {
    echo "ðŸ” Monitoring network health (Ctrl+C to stop)"
    echo ""
    while true; do
        clear
        echo "=== NETWORK HEALTH MONITOR ==="
        echo "Time: $(date)"
        echo ""
        
        # IP connectivity
        if ping -c 1 -W 1 8.8.8.8 &>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… Internet: OK"
        else
            echo "  âŒ Internet: DOWN"
        fi
        
        # DNS
        if nslookup google.com &>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… DNS: OK"
        else
            echo "  âŒ DNS: FAILED"
        fi
        
        # Gateway
        if [[ -n "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" ]] && ping -c 1 -W 1 "$DEFAULT_GATEWAY" &>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… Gateway: $DEFAULT_GATEWAY (reachable)"
        else
            echo "  âŒ Gateway: ${DEFAULT_GATEWAY:-'None'} (unreachable)"
        fi
        
        # Interface
        if [[ -n "$DEFAULT_IFACE" ]] && ip link show "$DEFAULT_IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "UP"; then
            echo "  âœ… Interface: $DEFAULT_IFACE (UP)"
        else
            echo "  âŒ Interface: ${DEFAULT_IFACE:-'None'} (DOWN)"
        fi
        
        echo ""
        echo "Press Ctrl+C to stop"
        sleep 2
    done
}

# ============================================================
# USAGE
# ============================================================

show_usage() {
    cat << EOF
Linux Network Recovery Engine v$VERSION

Usage:
    $SCRIPT_NAME diagnose   - Run full diagnostic (no changes)
    $SCRIPT_NAME repair     - Diagnose and attempt repairs
    $SCRIPT_NAME status     - Quick network health check
    $SCRIPT_NAME snapshot   - Save current network state
    $SCRIPT_NAME watch      - Monitor network health in real-time
    $SCRIPT_NAME help       - Show this help message

Examples:
    sudo network-recover diagnose
    sudo network-recover repair
    sudo network-recover status

Supported Distributions:
    Debian, Ubuntu, Fedora, Arch, MX Linux, RHEL, and derivatives

Features:
    âœ… Physical layer (carrier, link state)
    âœ… IP layer (IPv4, IPv6, duplicate IP detection)
    âœ… Routing (default routes, metrics, blackholes)
    âœ… Gateway (ARP, reachability)
    âœ… Internet (multiple targets, ICMP + TCP)
    âœ… DNS (resolution, poisoning detection, multiple servers)
    âœ… HTTPS/TLS (actual browsing simulation)
    âœ… NetworkManager (state, device status, stale connections)
    âœ… Virtualization (KVM/Libvirt, bridges, vnets)
    âœ… Kubernetes (optional, cluster connectivity)
    âœ… Captive portal detection
    âœ… MTU analysis
    âœ… ARP cache management
    âœ… Pre-repair snapshots

Report Issues: https://github.com/cypso05/Linux_Network-Recovery-Tool
EOF
}

# ============================================================
# MAIN
# ============================================================

main() {
    # Check for root privileges
    if [[ $EUID -ne 0 ]] && [[ "${1:-}" != "help" ]]; then
        echo "âŒ This script must be run as root (sudo)"
        echo "   Usage: sudo $SCRIPT_NAME ${1:-diagnose}"
        exit 1
    fi
    
    case "${1:-diagnose}" in
        diagnose|diag)
            cmd_diagnose
            ;;
        repair|fix)
            cmd_repair
            ;;
        status|stat)
            cmd_status
            ;;
        snapshot|snap)
            cmd_snapshot
            ;;
        watch|monitor)
            cmd_watch
            ;;
        help|--help|-h)
            show_usage
            ;;
        *)
            echo "âŒ Unknown command: ${1:-}"
            echo ""
            show_usage
            exit 1
            ;;
    esac
}

# ============================================================
# RUN
# ============================================================

main "$@"

#!/bin/bash
#===============================================================================
# Network Recovery Tool - System Dashboard
# Displays host and VM resource usage (disk, memory, load, K3s status)
# Output modes: text, json, html
#===============================================================================

set -euo pipefail

# Configuration - VM IPs to query
readonly VM_IPS=("10.0.0.21" "10.0.0.22" "10.0.0.23")
readonly VM_USER="${VM_USER:-devcyp}"
readonly SSH_TIMEOUT="${SSH_TIMEOUT:-3}"

# Output mode: text, json, or html
OUTPUT_MODE="${1:-text}"

#===============================================================================
# HOST RESOURCES
#===============================================================================
get_host_info() {
    local mem_total=$(free -h | grep Mem | awk '{print $2}')
    local mem_used=$(free -h | grep Mem | awk '{print $3}')
    local mem_avail=$(free -h | grep Mem | awk '{print $7}')
    local mem_percent=$(free | grep Mem | awk '{printf "%.0f", $3/$2*100}')
    local swap_total=$(free -h | grep Swap | awk '{print $2}')
    local swap_used=$(free -h | grep Swap | awk '{print $3}')
    local disk_info=$(df -h / | tail -1 | awk '{print $3 "/" $2 " (" $5 ")"}')
    local disk_percent=$(df / | tail -1 | awk '{print $5}' | tr -d '%')
    local load=$(uptime | awk -F'load average:' '{print $2}' | sed 's/^ *//')
    local hostname=$(hostname)

    case "$OUTPUT_MODE" in
        json)
            cat <<EOF
  "host": {
    "name": "$hostname",
    "memory": { "total": "$mem_total", "used": "$mem_used", "available": "$mem_avail", "percent": $mem_percent },
    "swap": { "total": "$swap_total", "used": "$swap_used" },
    "disk": { "info": "$disk_info", "percent": $disk_percent },
    "load": "$load"
  }
EOF
            ;;
        html)
            cat <<EOF
<div class='dashboard-section host-section'>
<h3>ðŸ–¥ï¸ Host: $hostname</h3>
<div class='resource-row'>
  <span class='resource-label'>Memory</span>
  <div class='resource-bar'><div class='resource-fill' style='width:${mem_percent}%'></div></div>
  <span class='resource-value'>${mem_used}/${mem_total} (${mem_percent}%)</span>
</div>
<div class='resource-row'>
  <span class='resource-label'>Swap</span>
  <span class='resource-value'>${swap_used}/${swap_total}</span>
</div>
<div class='resource-row'>
  <span class='resource-label'>Disk</span>
  <div class='resource-bar'><div class='resource-fill' style='width:${disk_percent}%'></div></div>
  <span class='resource-value'>${disk_info}</span>
</div>
<div class='resource-row'>
  <span class='resource-label'>Load</span>
  <span class='resource-value'>${load}</span>
</div>
</div>
EOF
            ;;
        *)
            cat <<EOF
=== HOST ($hostname) ===
Memory: ${mem_used}/${mem_total} (${mem_percent}%)
Swap:   ${swap_used}/${swap_total}
Disk:   ${disk_info}
Load:   ${load}
EOF
            ;;
    esac
}

#===============================================================================
# VM RESOURCES
#===============================================================================
get_vm_info() {
    case "$OUTPUT_MODE" in
        json) echo "  \"vms\": [" ;;
    esac

    local vm_count=0
    local first_vm=true

    for ip in "${VM_IPS[@]}"; do
        vm_count=$((vm_count + 1))
        local vm_output
        vm_output=$(ssh -o ConnectTimeout="$SSH_TIMEOUT" -o BatchMode=yes "$VM_USER@$ip" "
            echo \"MEM:\$(free -h | grep Mem | awk '{print \$3 \"/\" \$2 \" (\" int(\$3/\$2*100) \"%)\"'})\"
            echo \"DISK:\$(df -h / | tail -1 | awk '{print \$3 \"/\" \$2 \" (\" \$5 \")\"}')\"
            echo \"LOAD:\$(uptime | awk -F'load average:' '{print \$2}' | sed 's/^ *//')\"
        " 2>/dev/null || echo "MEM:N/A DISK:N/A LOAD:N/A")

        local vm_mem=$(echo "$vm_output" | grep "^MEM:" | cut -d: -f2-)
        local vm_disk=$(echo "$vm_output" | grep "^DISK:" | cut -d: -f2-)
        local vm_load=$(echo "$vm_output" | grep "^LOAD:" | cut -d: -f2-)

        case "$OUTPUT_MODE" in
            json)
                [[ "$first_vm" == true ]] && first_vm=false || echo ","
                cat <<EOF
    {
      "ip": "$ip",
      "memory": "$vm_mem",
      "disk": "$vm_disk",
      "load": "$vm_load"
    }
EOF
                ;;
            html)
                cat <<EOF
<div class='dashboard-section vm-section'>
<h3>ðŸ–¥ï¸ VM: $ip</h3>
<div class='resource-row'>
  <span class='resource-label'>Memory</span>
  <span class='resource-value'>$vm_mem</span>
</div>
<div class='resource-row'>
  <span class='resource-label'>Disk</span>
  <span class='resource-value'>$vm_disk</span>
</div>
<div class='resource-row'>
  <span class='resource-label'>Load</span>
  <span class='resource-value'>$vm_load</span>
</div>
</div>
EOF
                ;;
            *)
                echo ""
                echo "--- $ip ---"
                echo "Memory: $vm_mem"
                echo "Disk:   $vm_disk"
                echo "Load:   $vm_load"
                ;;
        esac
    done

    case "$OUTPUT_MODE" in
        json) echo "  ]" ;;
    esac
}

#===============================================================================
# K3s STATUS
#===============================================================================
get_k3s_info() {
    if ! command -v kubectl &>/dev/null; then
        case "$OUTPUT_MODE" in
            json) echo '  "k3s": { "status": "not_configured" }' ;;
            html) echo "<div class='k3s-section'><p>K3s not configured on this host.</p></div>" ;;
            *) echo "K3s: not configured" ;;
        esac
        return
    fi

    local nodes=$(kubectl get nodes --no-headers 2>/dev/null)
    local top_output=$(kubectl top nodes --no-headers 2>/dev/null || echo "")

    case "$OUTPUT_MODE" in
        json)
            echo '  "k3s": {'
            echo '    "status": "available",'
            echo '    "nodes": ['
            local first=true
            while IFS= read -r line; do
                [[ -z "$line" ]] && continue
                local name=$(echo "$line" | awk '{print $1}')
                local status=$(echo "$line" | awk '{print $2}')
                local role=$(echo "$line" | awk '{print $3}')
                local cpu=""
                local mem=""
                if [[ -n "$top_output" ]]; then
                    local top_line=$(echo "$top_output" | grep "^$name ")
                    cpu=$(echo "$top_line" | awk '{print $3}')
                    mem=$(echo "$top_line" | awk '{print $5}')
                fi
                [[ "$first" == true ]] && first=false || echo ","
                echo "      { \"name\": \"$name\", \"status\": \"$status\", \"role\": \"$role\", \"cpu\": \"${cpu:-N/A}\", \"memory\": \"${mem:-N/A}\" }"
            done <<< "$nodes"
            echo "    ]"
            echo "  }"
            ;;
        html)
            cat <<EOF
<div class='dashboard-section k3s-section'>
<h3>â˜¸ï¸ K3s Cluster</h3>
<table class='k3s-table'>
<tr><th>Node</th><th>Status</th><th>Role</th><th>CPU</th><th>Memory</th></tr>
EOF
            while IFS= read -r line; do
                [[ -z "$line" ]] && continue
                local name=$(echo "$line" | awk '{print $1}')
                local status=$(echo "$line" | awk '{print $2}')
                local role=$(echo "$line" | awk '{print $3}')
                local cpu=""
                local mem=""
                if [[ -n "$top_output" ]]; then
                    local top_line=$(echo "$top_output" | grep "^$name ")
                    cpu=$(echo "$top_line" | awk '{print $3}')
                    mem=$(echo "$top_line" | awk '{print $5}')
                fi
                echo "<tr><td>$name</td><td>$status</td><td>$role</td><td>${cpu:-N/A}</td><td>${mem:-N/A}</td></tr>"
            done <<< "$nodes"
            echo "</table></div>"
            ;;
        *)
            echo ""
            echo "=== K3s ==="
            echo "$nodes"
            echo ""
            if [[ -n "$top_output" ]]; then
                echo "$top_output"
            fi
            ;;
    esac
}

#===============================================================================
# MAIN
#===============================================================================
case "$OUTPUT_MODE" in
    json)
        echo "{"
        get_host_info
        echo ","
        get_vm_info
        echo ","
        get_k3s_info
        echo "}"
        ;;
    html)
        echo "<div class='dashboard'>"
        get_host_info
        get_vm_info
        get_k3s_info
        echo "</div>"
        ;;
    *)
        get_host_info
        get_vm_info
        get_k3s_info
        ;;
esac

#!/bin/bash
#===============================================================================
# Network Recovery Tool - Professional GUI v3.0.0
# Persistent window with embedded progress, dashboard, and status
#===============================================================================

readonly APP_NAME="Network Recovery"
readonly APP_VERSION="3.0.0"
readonly APP_ICON="network-recover"
readonly MAIN_WIDTH=680
readonly MAIN_HEIGHT=520
readonly DASHBOARD_CMD="/usr/local/bin/network-recover-dashboard"

#===============================================================================
# PROGRESS FEED (piped to zenity --progress)
#===============================================================================
run_action() {
    local action="$1"
    local title="$2"
    local log_file="/tmp/nrt-${action}-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S).log"

    # Start the command in background
    sudo network-recover "$action" > "$log_file" 2>&1 &
    local pid=$!

    # Stream progress markers from log file
    (
        local p=0
        echo "0" ; echo "# Starting $title..."
        while kill -0 $pid 2>/dev/null; do
            if [[ -f "$log_file" ]]; then
                [[ $p -lt 15 ]] && grep -q "LAYER 1:" "$log_file" 2>/dev/null && { p=15; echo "$p"; echo "# Physical layer..."; }
                [[ $p -lt 30 ]] && grep -q "LAYER 3:" "$log_file" 2>/dev/null && { p=30; echo "$p"; echo "# IP layer..."; }
                [[ $p -lt 50 ]] && grep -q "LAYER 5:" "$log_file" 2>/dev/null && { p=50; echo "$p"; echo "# Gateway..."; }
                [[ $p -lt 70 ]] && grep -q "LAYER 7:" "$log_file" 2>/dev/null && { p=70; echo "$p"; echo "# DNS..."; }
                [[ $p -lt 90 ]] && grep -q "REPAIR ENGINE" "$log_file" 2>/dev/null && { p=90; echo "$p"; echo "# Repairs..."; }
            fi
            sleep 0.3
        done
        wait $pid
        echo "100" ; echo "# Complete"
        sleep 0.5
    ) | zenity --progress \
        --title="$APP_NAME - $title" \
        --text="Working..." \
        --percentage=0 --auto-close --width=450 --no-cancel 2>/dev/null

    echo "$log_file"
}

#===============================================================================
# DASHBOARD
#===============================================================================
show_dashboard() {
    local tmp_html="/tmp/nrt-dashboard-$$.html"

    # Generate dashboard HTML with inline CSS
    cat > "$tmp_html" << 'DASHHTML'
<html>
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<style>
  body { font-family: -apple-system, sans-serif; background: #1a1a2e; color: #eee; margin: 20px; }
  h2 { color: #00d4ff; border-bottom: 1px solid #333; padding-bottom: 8px; }
  h3 { color: #7ec8e3; margin-top: 20px; }
  .row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; margin: 6px 0; }
  .label { width: 70px; font-size: 0.9em; color: #aaa; }
  .bar-bg { flex:1; height:14px; background:#2a2a3e; border-radius:7px; overflow:hidden; }
  .bar-fill { height:100%; border-radius:7px; transition: width .5s; }
  .bar-low { background: #4CAF50; }
  .bar-mid { background: #FFC107; }
  .bar-high { background: #f44336; }
  .value { min-width: 130px; font-size: 0.85em; text-align: right; }
  table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 10px; }
  th { text-align: left; color: #888; font-size: 0.8em; padding: 4px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #333; }
  td { padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 0.85em; border-bottom: 1px solid #222; }
  .vm-card { background: #16213e; border-radius: 8px; padding: 12px; margin: 10px 0; }
  .err { color: #f44336; text-align: center; padding: 40px; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<h2>📊 System Dashboard</h2>
<div id="content">Loading...</div>
<script>
async function load() {
  try {
    const r = await fetch('/api/dashboard');
    const d = await r.json();
    if (!d.ok) throw new Error(d.error);
    render(d);
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = '<div class="err">Dashboard unavailable.<br><small>Start the web server to see live data.</small></div>';
    // Fallback: try CLI
    try {
      const r2 = await fetch('/api/status');
      const s = await r2.json();
      if (s.ok) {
        document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = '<p>Web server is running. Ensure network-recover-dashboard is installed.</p><pre>' + s.output + '</pre>';
      }
    } catch(e2) {}
  }
}
function render(d) {
  let h = '';
  const memClass = d.host.memory.percent > 80 ? 'bar-high' : (d.host.memory.percent > 60 ? 'bar-mid' : 'bar-low');
  const diskClass = d.host.disk.percent > 80 ? 'bar-high' : (d.host.disk.percent > 60 ? 'bar-mid' : 'bar-low');
  h += '<h3>🖥️ Host: ' + esc(d.host.name) + '</h3>';
  h += row('Memory', d.host.memory.used+' / '+d.host.memory.total, d.host.memory.percent, memClass);
  h += row('Disk', d.host.disk.info, d.host.disk.percent, diskClass);
  h += '<div class="row"><span class="label">Load</span><span class="value" style="text-align:left">' + esc(d.host.load) + '</span></div>';
  if (d.vms) {
    d.vms.forEach(vm => {
      const mp = (vm.memory.match(/(\d+)%/)||[0,0])[1];
      const mc = mp > 80 ? 'bar-high' : (mp > 60 ? 'bar-mid' : 'bar-low');
      h += '<div class="vm-card"><h3>🖥️ VM: ' + esc(vm.ip) + '</h3>';
      h += row('Memory', vm.memory, mp, mc);
      h += '<div class="row"><span class="label">Disk</span><span class="value" style="text-align:left">' + esc(vm.disk) + '</span></div>';
      h += '<div class="row"><span class="label">Load</span><span class="value" style="text-align:left">' + esc(vm.load) + '</span></div>';
      h += '</div>';
    });
  }
  if (d.k3s && d.k3s.nodes) {
    h += '<h3>☸️ K3s Cluster</h3><table><tr><th>Node</th><th>Status</th><th>Role</th><th>CPU</th><th>Memory</th></tr>';
    d.k3s.nodes.forEach(n => {
      h += '<tr><td>'+esc(n.name)+'</td><td>'+esc(n.status)+'</td><td>'+esc(n.role)+'</td><td>'+esc(n.cpu||'N/A')+'</td><td>'+esc(n.memory||'N/A')+'</td></tr>';
    });
    h += '</table>';
  }
  document.getElementById('content').innerHTML = h;
}
function row(label, val, pct, cls) {
  return '<div class="row"><span class="label">'+label+'</span><div class="bar-bg"><div class="bar-fill '+cls+'" style="width:'+pct+'%"></div></div><span class="value">'+esc(val)+'</span></div>';
}
function esc(s) { const d=document.createElement('div'); d.textContent=s; return d.innerHTML; }
load();
</script>
</body>
</html>
DASHHTML

    # Try webview if available, fallback to zenity
    if command -v xdg-open &>/dev/null; then
        xdg-open "$tmp_html" 2>/dev/null &
    fi

    # Primary: use zenity text-info for CLI dashboard fallback
    local cli_output
    cli_output=$($DASHBOARD_CMD 2>/dev/null || echo "Dashboard unavailable. Install network-recover-dashboard.")
    
    zenity --text-info \
        --title="$APP_NAME - Dashboard" \
        --width=700 --height=550 \
        --font="Monospace 10" \
        --filename=<(echo "$cli_output") \
        2>/dev/null
}

#===============================================================================
# MAIN MENU (always visible, never disappears)
#===============================================================================
show_main_menu() {
    while true; do
        local choice
        choice=$(zenity --list \
            --title="$APP_NAME v$APP_VERSION" \
            --text="Select an action:" \
            --width=500 --height=400 \
            --column="Action" --column="Description" --column="Impact" \
            "📊 Status" "Quick network health overview" "🟢 Safe" \
            "🔍 Diagnose" "Full diagnostic - no changes" "🟢 Safe" \
            "🔧 Repair" "Diagnose and repair issues" "🟡 May disrupt" \
            "📈 Dashboard" "Host and VM resource monitor" "🟢 Safe" \
            "💾 Snapshot" "Save current network state" "🟢 Safe" \
            "📡 Monitor" "Real-time terminal monitor" "🟢 Safe" \
            "🚪 Quit" "Close this application" "" \
            2>/dev/null)

        [[ -z "$choice" ]] && exit 0

        case "$choice" in
            *Status*)
                local status_log
                status_log=$(run_action "status" "Network Status")
                zenity --text-info \
                    --title="$APP_NAME - Status" \
                    --width=700 --height=500 \
                    --filename="$status_log" \
                    2>/dev/null
                ;;
            *Diagnose*)
                local diag_log
                diag_log=$(run_action "diagnose" "Network Diagnosis")
                zenity --text-info \
                    --title="$APP_NAME - Diagnosis Report" \
                    --width=750 --height=550 \
                    --filename="$diag_log" \
                    2>/dev/null
                ;;
            *Repair*)
                zenity --question \
                    --title="$APP_NAME - Repair" \
                    --text="This will attempt to repair network issues.\n\nActive connections and VMs may be briefly disrupted.\n\nContinue?" \
                    --width=450 --ok-label="Repair Now" --cancel-label="Cancel" \
                    2>/dev/null || continue

                local repair_log
                repair_log=$(run_action "repair" "Network Repair")
                zenity --text-info \
                    --title="$APP_NAME - Repair Report" \
                    --width=750 --height=550 \
                    --filename="$repair_log" \
                    2>/dev/null
                ;;
            *Dashboard*)
                show_dashboard
                ;;
            *Snapshot*)
                local snap_log
                snap_log=$(run_action "snapshot" "Saving Snapshot")
                zenity --info \
                    --title="$APP_NAME - Snapshot" \
                    --text="✅ Snapshot saved successfully.\n\nLocation: /var/lib/network-recover/snapshots/" \
                    --width=400 \
                    2>/dev/null
                ;;
            *Monitor*)
                local term
                for t in xfce4-terminal gnome-terminal konsole xterm; do
                    command -v "$t" &>/dev/null && { term="$t"; break; }
                done
                if [[ -n "$term" ]]; then
                    "$term" -T "Network Monitor" -e "sudo network-recover watch" &
                else
                    zenity --error --text="No terminal emulator found." --width=300 2>/dev/null
                fi
                ;;
            *Quit*|"")
                exit 0
                ;;
        esac
    done
}

#===============================================================================
# ENTRY POINT
#===============================================================================
if [[ -n "${1:-}" ]]; then
    case "$1" in
        status)     run_action "status" "Status" > /dev/null; exit ;;
        diagnose)   run_action "diagnose" "Diagnose" > /dev/null; exit ;;
        repair)     run_action "repair" "Repair" > /dev/null; exit ;;
        dashboard)  show_dashboard; exit ;;
    esac
fi

show_main_menu

#!/bin/bash
# Launches the Network Recovery Tool web interface
readonly PORT=9876
readonly WEB_DIR="/usr/local/lib/network-recover/web"

# Get the original user (not root)
ORIGINAL_USER=${SUDO_USER:-$(whoami)}
ORIGINAL_HOME=$(eval echo ~$ORIGINAL_USER)

# Check if already running
if pgrep -f "python3.*server.py" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "Server is running, opening browser..."
    # Run Firefox as the original user, not root
    sudo -u $ORIGINAL_USER DISPLAY=:0 firefox "http://localhost:$PORT" 2>/dev/null &
    exit 0
fi

# Start server in background (needs root for network operations)
cd "$WEB_DIR"
python3 server.py --port "$PORT" --host 0.0.0.0 &
SERVER_PID=$!
sleep 2

# Open browser as the original user
echo "Opening browser..."
sudo -u $ORIGINAL_USER DISPLAY=:0 firefox "http://localhost:$PORT" 2>/dev/null &

# Wait for server
wait $SERVER_PID

#!/bin/bash
# tests/test-offline.sh - Simulate offline conditions and test recovery
# Usage: ./test-offline.sh [--diagnose-only] [--interface IFACE]

set -euo pipefail

# Parse arguments
DIAGNOSE_ONLY=false
TEST_IFACE=""
REPAIR_TIMEOUT=60  # seconds to wait for repair before forcing restoration

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
    case $1 in
        --diagnose-only|-d)
            DIAGNOSE_ONLY=true
            shift
            ;;
        --interface|-i)
            TEST_IFACE="$2"
            shift 2
            ;;
        --timeout|-t)
            REPAIR_TIMEOUT="$2"
            shift 2
            ;;
        --help|-h)
            echo "Usage: $0 [--diagnose-only] [--interface IFACE] [--timeout SECONDS]"
            echo "  --diagnose-only  Run diagnose only (no repairs)"
            echo "  --interface      Specify interface to test (default: auto-detect)"
            echo "  --timeout        Timeout for repair command (default: 60s)"
            echo "  --help           Show this help"
            exit 0
            ;;
        *)
            echo "Unknown option: $1"
            echo "Use --help for usage"
            exit 1
            ;;
    esac
done

# Determine which network-recover binary to use
find_network_recover() {
    if command -v network-recover &>/dev/null; then
        echo "network-recover"
        return 0
    fi
    if [[ -f "./src/network-recover" ]] && [[ -x "./src/network-recover" ]]; then
        echo "./src/network-recover"
        return 0
    fi
    if [[ -f "./src/network-recover" ]]; then
        chmod +x ./src/network-recover
        echo "./src/network-recover"
        return 0
    fi
    echo ""
    return 1
}

NETWORK_RECOVER=$(find_network_recover)
if [[ -z "$NETWORK_RECOVER" ]]; then
    echo "âŒ network-recover not found!"
    echo "   Please install with: sudo ./install.sh"
    echo "   Or run from the repo root: cd ~/Linux_Network-Recovery-Tool"
    exit 1
fi

echo "ðŸ“Œ Using: $NETWORK_RECOVER"
echo ""

echo "=============================================="
echo "  NETWORK OFFLINE TEST"
echo "=============================================="
echo ""

# Detect interface
if [[ -z "$TEST_IFACE" ]]; then
    IFACE=$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $5; exit}' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    if [[ -z "$IFACE" ]]; then
        # Fallback: find any interface with an IP
        IFACE=$(ip -4 addr show 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '^[0-9]+: \K[^:]+' | grep -v lo | head -1)
    fi
    [[ -z "$IFACE" ]] && IFACE="eth0"
else
    IFACE="$TEST_IFACE"
fi

echo "ðŸ“Œ Interface: $IFACE"

# Determine interface type
if [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$IFACE/wireless" ]] || [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$IFACE/phy80211" ]]; then
    IFACE_TYPE="wireless"
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Type: WiFi"
elif [[ -d "/sys/class/net/$IFACE/bridge" ]] || ip link show "$IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "bridge"; then
    IFACE_TYPE="bridge"
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Type: Bridge"
else
    IFACE_TYPE="ethernet"
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Type: Ethernet"
fi

# Check if interface exists
if [[ ! -d "/sys/class/net/$IFACE" ]]; then
    echo "âŒ Interface $IFACE does not exist"
    exit 1
fi

# Save current state
echo ""
echo "ðŸ“Œ Current state:"
if ip link show "$IFACE" | grep -q "UP"; then
    echo "  âœ… Interface is UP"
else
    echo "  âš ï¸  Interface is DOWN"
fi

# Use \K instead of lookbehind for grep portability
IP_ADDR=$(ip -4 addr show "$IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'inet\s\K[0-9.]+' | head -1)
echo "  IP: ${IP_ADDR:-None}"

GATEWAY=$(ip route | awk '/default/ {print $3; exit}' 2>/dev/null || echo "None")
echo "  Gateway: $GATEWAY"

# Save connection name if using NetworkManager
CONN_NAME=""
if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
    CONN_NAME=$(nmcli -t -f NAME,DEVICE connection show --active 2>/dev/null | grep ":${IFACE}$" | cut -d: -f1)
    if [[ -n "$CONN_NAME" ]]; then
        echo "  Connection: $CONN_NAME"
    fi
fi

# Detect if VMs are running on this bridge (informational only)
VM_COUNT=0
if [[ "$IFACE_TYPE" == "bridge" ]] && command -v virsh &>/dev/null; then
    VM_COUNT=$(virsh list --state-running --name 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    if [[ "$VM_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  $VM_COUNT VM(s) detected on this bridge"
    fi
fi

# Test connectivity before disabling
echo ""
echo "ðŸ“Œ Testing connectivity before disabling..."
if ping -c 1 -W 2 8.8.8.8 &>/dev/null; then
    echo "  âœ… Internet reachable"
else
    echo "  âš ï¸  Internet not reachable (test may be less useful)"
fi

echo ""
echo "=============================================="
echo "  SIMULATING OFFLINE CONDITIONS"
echo "=============================================="
echo ""

# Disable interface
echo "ðŸ“Œ Disabling $IFACE..."
if [[ "$IFACE_TYPE" == "wireless" ]]; then
    if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
        sudo nmcli radio wifi off 2>/dev/null && echo "  âœ… WiFi disabled (nmcli)"
    else
        sudo ip link set "$IFACE" down 2>/dev/null && echo "  âœ… Interface down (ip link)"
    fi
else
    sudo ip link set "$IFACE" down 2>/dev/null && echo "  âœ… Interface down (ip link)"
fi

# Verify it's down
sleep 2
if ip link show "$IFACE" | grep -q "UP"; then
    echo "  âš ï¸  Interface still UP - forced down may have failed"
fi

echo ""
echo "ðŸ“Œ Verifying network is down..."
if ping -c 1 -W 2 8.8.8.8 &>/dev/null; then
    echo "  âš ï¸  Internet still reachable (test may not work)"
else
    echo "  âœ… Internet unreachable (as expected)"
fi

echo ""
echo "=============================================="
echo "  RUNNING NETWORK RECOVERY"
echo "=============================================="
echo ""

# Guard to prevent restore_network from running twice
RESTORE_DONE=false

# Function to restore network regardless of repair outcome
restore_network() {
    # Prevent double execution
    if [[ "$RESTORE_DONE" == "true" ]]; then
        return 0
    fi
    RESTORE_DONE=true
    
    echo ""
    echo "=============================================="
    echo "  RESTORING NETWORK"
    echo "=============================================="
    echo ""
    
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Re-enabling $IFACE..."
    if [[ "$IFACE_TYPE" == "wireless" ]]; then
        if command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
            sudo nmcli radio wifi on 2>/dev/null && echo "  âœ… WiFi enabled (nmcli)"
        else
            sudo ip link set "$IFACE" up 2>/dev/null && echo "  âœ… Interface up (ip link)"
        fi
    else
        sudo ip link set "$IFACE" up 2>/dev/null && echo "  âœ… Interface up (ip link)"
    fi
    
    echo "  â³ Waiting for interface to stabilize..."
    sleep 3
    
    if [[ -n "$CONN_NAME" ]] && command -v nmcli &>/dev/null; then
        echo "ðŸ“Œ Re-activating connection '$CONN_NAME'..."
        sudo nmcli con down "$CONN_NAME" 2>/dev/null || true
        sleep 1
        if sudo nmcli con up "$CONN_NAME" 2>/dev/null; then
            echo "  âœ… Connection re-activated"
        else
            echo "  âš ï¸  Failed to re-activate connection"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Brief wait for bridge to stabilize (just a moment, not minutes)
    echo "  â³ Waiting for bridge to stabilize..."
    sleep 2
    
    # Show network status
    echo ""
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Network Status:"
    
    # Check interface
    if ip link show "$IFACE" | grep -q "UP"; then
        echo "  âœ… $IFACE is UP"
    else
        echo "  âŒ $IFACE is DOWN"
    fi
    
    # Check IP
    BRIDGE_IP=$(ip -4 addr show "$IFACE" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'inet\s\K[0-9.]+' | head -1)
    if [[ -n "$BRIDGE_IP" ]]; then
        echo "  âœ… IP: $BRIDGE_IP"
    else
        echo "  âŒ No IP assigned"
    fi
    
    # Check internet
    if ping -c 1 -W 2 8.8.8.8 &>/dev/null; then
        echo "  âœ… Internet connectivity restored"
        INTERNET_RESTORED=true
    else
        echo "  â³ Internet connectivity pending (may take a moment)"
        INTERNET_RESTORED=false
    fi
    
    # Generic VM info (if any VMs exist)
    if [[ "$IFACE_TYPE" == "bridge" ]] && command -v virsh &>/dev/null; then
        local running_vms=$(virsh list --state-running --name 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
        if [[ "$running_vms" -gt 0 ]]; then
            echo ""
            echo "ðŸ“Œ Virtual Machines:"
            echo "  â„¹ï¸  $running_vms VM(s) are running"
            echo "  â„¹ï¸  VMs will reconnect to the bridge automatically"
            echo "  â„¹ï¸  This may take 30-60 seconds for VMs to get network"
            echo ""
            echo "  To check VM network status:"
            echo "    sudo virsh list --state-running --name | while read vm; do"
            echo "      echo \"\$vm: \$(sudo virsh domifaddr \"\$vm\" 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'ipv4\\s+\\K[0-9.]+')\""
            echo "    done"
        else
            echo ""
            echo "ðŸ“Œ Virtual Machines:"
            echo "  â„¹ï¸  No VMs detected on this bridge"
            echo "  â„¹ï¸  Start VMs with: sudo virsh start <vm-name>"
        fi
    fi
    
    # Final user guidance
    echo ""
    echo "=============================================="
    echo "  NETWORK RESTORATION COMPLETE"
    echo "=============================================="
    echo ""
    echo "âœ… Internet connectivity is restored"
    echo ""
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Next Steps:"
    echo "  1. Test browsing: ping -c 3 google.com"
    echo "  2. Check status: sudo $NETWORK_RECOVER status"
    echo ""
    if [[ "$IFACE_TYPE" == "bridge" ]] && [[ "$VM_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  VMs will reconnect shortly"
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  Check VM connectivity with: sudo virsh domifaddr <vm-name>"
        echo "  â„¹ï¸  If VMs don't have network after 2 minutes, restart them:"
        echo "      sudo virsh reboot <vm-name>"
    fi
    echo "=============================================="
}

# Trap to ensure restoration even if script is interrupted (Ctrl+C)
trap restore_network EXIT

if [[ "$DIAGNOSE_ONLY" == "true" ]]; then
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Running diagnose only (no repairs)..."
    sudo $NETWORK_RECOVER diagnose
else
    echo "ðŸ“Œ Running repair (timeout: ${REPAIR_TIMEOUT}s)..."
    
    # Set the interface override for the repair
    export NETWORK_RECOVER_IFACE="$IFACE"
    
    # Run repair with timeout to prevent hanging on DNS repairs
    # -E preserves the environment variable through sudo
    timeout "$REPAIR_TIMEOUT" sudo -E $NETWORK_RECOVER repair
    REPAIR_EXIT=$?
    if [[ $REPAIR_EXIT -eq 124 ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  Repair timed out after ${REPAIR_TIMEOUT}s"
    elif [[ $REPAIR_EXIT -ne 0 ]]; then
        echo "  âš ï¸  Repair exited with code $REPAIR_EXIT"
    else
        echo "  âœ… Repair completed successfully"
    fi
fi

# Remove trap to avoid duplicate restoration on normal exit
trap - EXIT

# Now explicitly restore
restore_network

# Final verification
echo ""
echo "=============================================="
echo "  FINAL VERIFICATION"
echo "=============================================="
echo ""

echo "ðŸ“Œ Testing connectivity..."
if ping -c 2 -W 2 8.8.8.8 &>/dev/null; then
    echo "  âœ… Internet: CONNECTED"
    echo "  âœ… Test PASSED - Network is working"
    echo ""
    echo "  You can now:"
    echo "    - Browse the internet"
    echo "    - Check VMs if applicable"
    echo "    - Run: sudo $NETWORK_RECOVER status"
    exit 0
else
    echo "  â³ Internet: PENDING (may take a moment)"
    echo "  ðŸ’¡ If internet doesn't come back:"
    echo "    sudo $NETWORK_RECOVER repair"
    echo "    sudo $NETWORK_RECOVER status"
    exit 1
fi
