                        <div class="sparkline" id="cpu-sparkline"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-label">RAM</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold;" id="ram-value">${m?.ram || 0}%</div>
                        <div class="sparkline" id="ram-sparkline"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-label">Network</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold;">${m?.network_rx_mbps || 0} Mbps</div>
                        <div class="sparkline" id="net-sparkline"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-label">Disk</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold;">${m?.disk || 0}%</div>
                        <div class="sparkline" id="disk-sparkline"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                <!-- Events & Tasks -->
                <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px;">
                    <div class="panel">
                        <div class="panel-header">Recent Events</div>
                        <div class="panel-body" style="max-height: 300px; overflow-y: auto;" id="overview-events">
                            ${(state.events || []).slice(0, 10).map(e => `
                                <div class="timeline-item">
                                    <span class="timeline-time">${new Date(e.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                                    <span class="timeline-icon ${e.severity}">${e.severity === 'success' ? 'âœ“' : e.severity === 'warning' ? 'âš ' : e.severity === 'error' ? 'âœ—' : 'â„¹'}</span>
                                    <span>${e.message}</span>
                                </div>
                            `).join('') || '<div style="color:#6a7090;font-size:12px;">No events</div>'}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="panel">
                        <div class="panel-header">Current Jobs</div>
                        <div class="panel-body" style="max-height: 300px; overflow-y: auto;" id="overview-jobs">
                            <div style="color:#6a7090;font-size:12px;">No active jobs</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            
            // Request sparkline data
            sendCommand('get_metrics_history', { period: '1h' });
        }

        function renderVMList() {
            const vms = state.vms || [];
            const k8s = state.kubernetes || {};
            const pods = k8s.pods || [];
            
            document.getElementById('content-area').innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel">
                    <div class="panel-header">Virtual Machines <span style="color:#6a7090;font-weight:normal;">(${vms.length} total)</span></div>

                                    <th>Actions</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                ${vms.map(vm => {
                                    const podCount = pods.filter(p => p.node === vm.name).length;
                                    const isPaused = vm.state === 'paused';
                                    const isRunning = vm.state === 'running';
                                    return `
                                    <tr class="${state.selectedItems.has('vm:'+vm.name) ? 'selected' : ''}" onclick="openDrawer('vm', '${vm.name}')">
                                        <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()"><input type="checkbox" ${state.selectedItems.has('vm:'+vm.name) ? 'checked' : ''} onclick="toggleSelectItem('vm:${vm.name}')"></td>
                                        <td style="color:#64b5f6;">${vm.name}</td>
                                        <td><span class="badge ${isRunning ? 'badge-success' : isPaused ? 'badge-warning' : 'badge-error'}">${vm.state}</span></td>
                                        <td>${vm.ip || 'unknown'}</td>
                                        <td>${vm.cpu || 'N/A'}</td>
                                        <td>${vm.ram || 'N/A'}</td>
                                        <td>${podCount}</td>
                                        <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
                                            <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${vm.name}', 'restart')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Restart">â–¶</button>
                                            <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${vm.name}', 'shutdown')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Shutdown">â¹</button>
                                            <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${vm.name}', 'snapshot')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Snapshot">ðŸ’¾</button>
                                            ${isRunning ? `<button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${vm.name}', 'pause')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Pause">â¸</button>` : ''}
                                            ${isPaused ? `<button class="btn btn-success" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${vm.name}', 'resume')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Resume">â–¶ï¸</button>` : ''}
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                `}).join('')}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        function renderK8sView(type) {
            const k8s = state.kubernetes || {};
            let data = [];
            let title = '';
            let extraColumns = '';
            let rowActions = (item) => '';
            
            switch(type) {
                case 'k8s-nodes':
                    data = k8s.nodes || [];
                    title = 'Nodes';
                    extraColumns = `<th>Version</th><th>CPU</th><th>Memory</th>`;
                    rowActions = (item) => `
                        <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('node', '${item.name}', 'cordon')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Cordon">â›”</button>
                        <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('node', '${item.name}', 'uncordon')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Uncordon">âœ…</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('node', '${item.name}', 'drain')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Drain">ðŸ’§</button>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'k8s-pods':
                    data = k8s.pods || [];
                    title = 'Pods';
                    extraColumns = `<th>Node</th><th>Restarts</th>`;
                    rowActions = (item) => `
                        <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('pod', '${item.name}', 'restart')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Restart">â–¶</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('pod', '${item.name}', 'delete')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Delete">ðŸ—‘</button>
                        <button class="btn" onclick="showPodLogs('${item.namespace}', '${item.name}')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Logs">ðŸ“‹</button>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'k8s-deployments':
                    data = k8s.deployments || [];
                    title = 'Deployments';
                    extraColumns = ``;
                    rowActions = (item) => `
                        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="scaleDeployment('${item.name}')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Scale">ðŸ“ˆ</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('deployment', '${item.name}', 'delete')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Delete">ðŸ—‘</button>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'k8s-services':
                    data = k8s.services || [];
                    title = 'Services';
                    extraColumns = ``;
                    rowActions = (item) => `
                        <button class="btn" onclick="showNotification('Service', 'Details for ${item.name}', 'info')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Details">â„¹</button>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'k8s-jobs':
                    data = (k8s.jobs || []).concat(k8s.cronjobs || []);
                    title = 'Jobs';
                    extraColumns = ``;
                    rowActions = (item) => `
                        <button class="btn" onclick="showNotification('Job', 'Logs for ${item.name}', 'info')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;" title="Logs">ðŸ“‹</button>
                    `;
                    break;
                default:
                    data = [];
                    title = 'Unknown';
            }
            
            document.getElementById('content-area').innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel">
                    <div class="panel-header">Kubernetes ${title} <span style="color:#6a7090;font-weight:normal;">(${data.length})</span></div>
                    <div class="panel-body" style="padding:0;">
                        <table class="data-table">
                            <thead>
                                <tr>
                                    <th style="width:30px;"><input type="checkbox" onclick="toggleSelectAll(this, 'k8s')"></th>
                                    <th>Name</th>
                                    <th>Namespace</th>
                                    <th>Status</th>
                                    ${extraColumns}
                                    <th>Age</th>
                                    <th>Actions</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                ${data.map(item => `
                                    <tr class="${state.selectedItems.has('k8s:'+item.name) ? 'selected' : ''}" onclick="openDrawer('${type}', '${item.name}')">
                                        <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()"><input type="checkbox" ${state.selectedItems.has('k8s:'+item.name) ? 'checked' : ''} onclick="toggleSelectItem('k8s:${item.name}')"></td>
                                        <td style="color:#64b5f6;">${item.name}</td>
                                        <td>${item.namespace || '-'}</td>
                                        <td><span class="badge ${getK8sStatusBadge(item.status || item.state)}">${item.status || item.state || '-'}</span></td>
                                        ${type === 'k8s-nodes' ? `<td>${item.version || '-'}</td><td>${item.cpu || '-'}</td><td>${item.memory || '-'}</td>` : ''}
                                        ${type === 'k8s-pods' ? `<td>${item.node || '-'}</td><td>${item.restarts || 0}</td>` : ''}
                                        <td>${item.age || '-'}</td>
                                        <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
                                            ${rowActions(item)}
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                `).join('')}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        function renderProblems(problems) {
            problems = problems || [];
            document.getElementById('content-area').innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel">
                    <div class="panel-header">Detected Problems <span style="color:#6a7090;font-weight:normal;">(${problems.length})</span></div>
                    <div class="panel-body">
                        ${problems.length === 0 ? '<div style="color:#6a7090;font-size:12px;padding:20px;text-align:center;">âœ… No problems detected</div>' : ''}
                        ${problems.map(p => `
                            <div style="background:#0d1130;padding:14px;border-radius:4px;margin-bottom:10px;border-left:3px solid ${p.severity === 'critical' ? '#f44336' : '#ff9800'};">
                                <div style="font-weight:bold;color:${p.severity === 'critical' ? '#f44336' : '#ff9800'};margin-bottom:6px;">${p.severity === 'critical' ? 'ðŸ”´' : 'ðŸŸ¡'} ${p.message}</div>
                                <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8890b0;margin-bottom:10px;">${p.suggested_action || ''}</div>
                                <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="runRepair('${p.message}')">ðŸ”§ Fix</button>
                                <button class="btn" onclick="runDiagnostics()">ðŸ” Diagnose</button>
                                <button class="btn">Ignore</button>
                            </div>
                        `).join('')}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }

                            </label>
                        </div>

        function renderTimeline(data) {
            data = data || [];
            document.getElementById('content-area').innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel">
                    <div class="panel-header">Event Timeline</div>
                    <div class="panel-body" style="max-height:600px;overflow-y:auto;">
                        ${data.map(e => `
                            <div class="timeline-item">
                                <span class="timeline-time">${new Date(e.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                                <span class="timeline-icon ${e.severity}">${e.severity === 'success' ? 'âœ“' : e.severity === 'warning' ? 'âš ' : 'âœ—'}</span>
                                <div>
                                    <div style="font-weight:500;">${e.type.toUpperCase()}</div>
                                    <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8890b0;">${e.message}</div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        `).join('') || '<div style="color:#6a7090;padding:20px;text-align:center;">No events in timeline</div>'}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        function renderEvents() {
            document.getElementById('content-area').innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel">
                    <div class="panel-header">Live Event Stream</div>
                    <div class="panel-body" style="max-height:600px;overflow-y:auto;" id="live-event-stream">
                        ${(state.events || []).map(e => `
                            <div class="timeline-item">
                                <span class="timeline-time">${new Date(e.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                                <span class="timeline-icon ${e.severity}">${e.severity === 'success' ? 'âœ“' : e.severity === 'warning' ? 'âš ' : 'âœ—'}</span>
                                <span>${e.message}</span>
                            </div>
                        `).join('')}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        // ============================================================
        // DRAWER
        // ============================================================
        function openDrawer(type, name) {
            const drawer = document.getElementById('drawer');
            const overlay = document.getElementById('drawer-overlay');
            const title = document.getElementById('drawer-title');
            const body = document.getElementById('drawer-body');
            const actions = document.getElementById('drawer-actions');
            
            title.textContent = name;
            
            if (type === 'vm') {
                // Show loading state
                body.innerHTML = `<div style="color:#6a7090;text-align:center;padding:40px;">Loading details for ${name}...</div>`;
                actions.innerHTML = `
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${name}', 'restart')">â–¶ Restart</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${name}', 'shutdown')">â¹ Shutdown</button>
                    <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${name}', 'snapshot')">ðŸ’¾ Snapshot</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${name}', 'pause')">â¸ Pause</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-success" onclick="executeAction('vm', '${name}', 'resume')">â–¶ï¸ Resume</button>
                `;
                
                // Fetch VM details
                state._pendingDrawer = { type: 'vm', name: name };
                sendCommand('get_vm_details', { name: name });
                
            } else if (type === 'k8s-nodes') {
                body.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="margin-bottom:16px;">
                        <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8890b0;margin-bottom:4px;">STATUS</div>
                        <span class="badge badge-success">Ready</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>Node:</strong> ${name}</div>
                `;
                actions.innerHTML = `
                    <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('node', '${name}', 'cordon')">â›” Cordon</button>
                    <button class="btn" onclick="executeAction('node', '${name}', 'uncordon')">âœ… Uncordon</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('node', '${name}', 'drain')">ðŸ’§ Drain</button>
                `;
            } else if (type === 'k8s-pods') {
                const pod = (state.kubernetes?.pods || []).find(p => p.name === name);
                body.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="margin-bottom:16px;">
                        <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8890b0;margin-bottom:4px;">STATUS</div>
                        <span class="badge ${getK8sStatusBadge(pod?.status)}">${pod?.status || 'Unknown'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>Name:</strong> ${name}</div>
                    <div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>Namespace:</strong> ${pod?.namespace || '-'}</div>
                    <div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>Node:</strong> ${pod?.node || '-'}</div>
                    <div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>Restarts:</strong> ${pod?.restarts || 0}</div>
                    <div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>Age:</strong> ${pod?.age || '-'}</div>
                `;
                actions.innerHTML = `
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="executeAction('pod', '${name}', 'restart')">â–¶ Restart</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('pod', '${name}', 'delete')">ðŸ—‘ Delete</button>
                    <button class="btn" onclick="showPodLogs('${pod?.namespace || 'default'}', '${name}')">ðŸ“‹ Logs</button>
                `;
            } else if (type === 'k8s-deployments') {
                body.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>Deployment:</strong> ${name}</div>
                `;
                actions.innerHTML = `
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="scaleDeployment('${name}')">ðŸ“ˆ Scale</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="executeAction('deployment', '${name}', 'delete')">ðŸ—‘ Delete</button>
                `;
            } else {
                body.innerHTML = `<div>Details for ${type}: ${name}</div>`;
                actions.innerHTML = '';
            }
            
            drawer.classList.add('open');
            overlay.classList.add('open');
        }
        
        function renderVMDrawerDetails(details) {
            const body = document.getElementById('drawer-body');
            if (!body) return;
            
            const podsOnNode = (state.kubernetes?.pods || []).filter(p => p.node === details.name);
            
            body.innerHTML = `
                <div style="margin-bottom:16px;">
                    <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8890b0;margin-bottom:4px;">STATUS</div>
                    <span class="badge ${details.state === 'running' ? 'badge-success' : details.state === 'paused' ? 'badge-warning' : 'badge-error'}">${details.state}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="cards-grid" style="grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;">
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-label">vCPUs</div>
                        <div style="font-size:18px;">${details.vcpus || 'N/A'}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-label">Memory</div>
                        <div style="font-size:18px;">${details.memory?.used || 'N/A'}</div>
                        <div class="card-detail">Max: ${details.memory?.max || 'N/A'}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-label">Disks</div>
                        <div style="font-size:18px;">${details.disks?.length || 0}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-label">Interfaces</div>
                        <div style="font-size:18px;">${details.interfaces?.length || 0}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                
                ${podsOnNode.length > 0 ? `
                    <div style="margin-top:16px;">
                        <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8890b0;margin-bottom:8px;">ðŸ“¦ PODS ON THIS NODE (${podsOnNode.length})</div>
                        ${podsOnNode.map(p => `
                            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-size:12px;">
                                <span style="color:#64b5f6;cursor:pointer;" onclick="openDrawer('k8s-pods', '${p.name}')">${p.name}</span>
                                <span class="badge ${getK8sStatusBadge(p.status)}">${p.status}</span>
                            </div>
                        `).join('')}
                    </div>
                ` : ''}
                
                ${details.snapshots?.length > 0 ? `
                    <div style="margin-top:16px;">
                        <div style="font-size:11px;color:#8890b0;margin-bottom:8px;">ðŸ’¾ SNAPSHOTS</div>
                        ${details.snapshots.map(s => `
                            <div style="font-size:12px;padding:4px 0;color:var(--text-secondary);">${s}</div>
                        `).join('')}
                    </div>
                ` : ''}
            `;
        }
        
        function closeDrawer() {
            document.getElementById('drawer').classList.remove('open');
            document.getElementById('drawer-overlay').classList.remove('open');
            state._pendingDrawer = null;
        }
        
        // ============================================================
        // NEW FUNCTIONS FOR SCALING, PAUSE/RESUME, NODE ACTIONS
        // ============================================================
        function scaleDeployment(name) {
            const replicas = prompt(`Enter new replica count for deployment "${name}":`, "3");
            if (replicas === null) return;
            const num = parseInt(replicas);
            if (isNaN(num) || num < 0) {
                alert("Please enter a valid number");
                return;
            }
            sendCommand('execute_action', {
                resource_type: 'deployment',
                resource_name: name,
                action: 'scale',
                replicas: num
            });
            showNotification('Scale', `Scaling ${name} to ${num} replicas`, 'info');
        }
        
        function showPodLogs(namespace, name) {
            // This would need a real terminal/log viewer implementation
            // For now, just show a notification
            showNotification('Logs', `Viewing logs for ${namespace}/${name}`, 'info');
            // In a real implementation, you'd open a new panel or fetch logs
            alert(`Logs for ${namespace}/${name}\n\n(Log viewer would appear here - implement with kubectl logs)`);
        }
        
        // ============================================================
        // ACTIONS
        // ============================================================
        function refreshAll() {
            sendCommand('get_metrics');
            sendCommand('get_vms');
            sendCommand('get_kubernetes');
            sendCommand('get_events');
            sendCommand('get_alerts');
            sendCommand('get_problems');
        }
        
        function runDiagnostics() {
            sendCommand('run_diagnostics');
            showNotification('Diagnostics', 'Running...', 'info');
        }
        
        function runRepair(target) {
            sendCommand('run_repair', { target: target || '' });
            showNotification('Repair', 'Starting repair...', 'info');
        }
        
        function createSnapshot() {
            const vmName = prompt('Enter VM name for snapshot:');
            if (vmName) {
                sendCommand('execute_action', { resource_type: 'vm', resource_name: vmName, action: 'snapshot' });
            }
        }
        
        function executeAction(type, name, action) {
            if (!name || name === '') {
                showNotification('Error', 'Resource name is required', 'error');
                return;
            }
            sendCommand('execute_action', { resource_type: type, resource_name: name, action: action });
            showNotification('Action', `${action} on ${type} ${name} started`, 'info');
        }
        
        function emergencyMode() {
            if (confirm('âš  EMERGENCY MODE - This will restart failed pods, repair networking, and flush caches. Continue?')) {
                runRepair('all');
                showNotification('Emergency', 'Emergency mode activated', 'warning');
            }
        }
        
        // ============================================================
        // SEARCH
        // ============================================================
        function handleSearch(query) {
            if (query.length >= 2) {
                sendCommand('search', { query });
            } else {
                document.getElementById('search-results').classList.remove('show');
            }
        }
        
        function renderSearchResults(results) {
            const container = document.getElementById('search-results');
            if (!results || results.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="search-result-item" style="color:#6a7090;">No results</div>';
            } else {
                container.innerHTML = results.map(r => `
                    <div class="search-result-item" onclick="navigateToResource('${r.path}')">
                        <span>${r.name}</span>
                        <span class="type-badge">${r.type}</span>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
            }
            container.classList.add('show');
        }
        
        function navigateToResource(path) {
            document.getElementById('search-results').classList.remove('show');
            document.getElementById('global-search').value = '';
            // Navigate based on path
            if (path.startsWith('vm/')) {
                selectResource('vms');
            } else if (path.startsWith('kubernetes/')) {
                const parts = path.split('/');
                if (parts.length >= 3) {
                    selectResource('k8s-' + parts[1]);
                }
            } else if (path === 'host') {
                selectResource('host');
            }
        }
        
        // ============================================================
        // UTILITIES
        // ============================================================
        function updateMetricsDisplay(m) {
            const cpuEl = document.getElementById('cpu-value');
            const ramEl = document.getElementById('ram-value');
            if (cpuEl) cpuEl.textContent = (m?.cpu || 0) + '%';
            if (ramEl) ramEl.textContent = (m?.ram || 0) + '%';
        }
        
        function updateStatusBar() {
            const elapsed = ((Date.now() - state.lastUpdate) / 1000).toFixed(1);
            document.getElementById('sb-last-update').textContent = elapsed + 's ago';
            
            const vms = state.vms || [];
            const k8s = state.kubernetes || {};
            document.getElementById('sb-vms').textContent = vms.length + ' VMs';
            document.getElementById('sb-pods').textContent = (k8s.pods || []).length + ' Pods';
            document.getElementById('sb-services').textContent = (k8s.services || []).length + ' Services';
        }
        
        function updateSidebarCounts() {
            const vms = state.vms || [];
            const k8s = state.kubernetes || {};
            document.getElementById('vm-count-sidebar').textContent = vms.length;
            document.getElementById('node-count-sidebar').textContent = (k8s.nodes || []).length;
            document.getElementById('pod-count-sidebar').textContent = (k8s.pods || []).length;
            document.getElementById('problem-count-sidebar').textContent = state.alerts.filter(a => !a.acknowledged).length;
        }
        
        function updateAlertBell() {
            const count = state.alerts.filter(a => !a.acknowledged).length;
            const badge = document.getElementById('alert-count');
            if (count > 0) {
                badge.textContent = count;
                badge.style.display = 'flex';
            } else {
                badge.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }
        
        function toggleAlerts() {
            const dropdown = document.getElementById('alerts-dropdown');
            dropdown.innerHTML = state.alerts.map(a => `
                <div class="alert-item" style="border-left:3px solid ${a.severity === 'critical' ? '#f44336' : '#ff9800'};">
                    <div style="font-weight:bold;">${a.severity.toUpperCase()}</div>
                    <div style="font-size:11px;">${a.message}</div>
                    <div style="font-size:10px;color:#6a7090;">${new Date(a.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</div>
                    ${!a.acknowledged ? `<button class="btn" onclick="acknowledgeAlert('${a.id}')" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;margin-top:4px;">âœ“ Acknowledge</button>` : '<span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--success);">âœ“ Acknowledged</span>'}
                </div>
            `).join('') || '<div class="alert-item" style="color:#6a7090;">No alerts</div>';
            dropdown.classList.toggle('show');
        }
        
        function acknowledgeAlert(alertId) {
            sendCommand('acknowledge_alert', { alert_id: alertId });
            toggleAlerts();
        }
        
        function showNotification(title, message, type) {
            console.log(`[${type}] ${title}: ${message}`);
            // Toast notification would go here
        }
        
        function getK8sStatusBadge(status) {
            const s = (status || '').toLowerCase();
            if (s.includes('running') || s.includes('ready') || s.includes('bound')) return 'badge-success';
            if (s.includes('pending') || s.includes('waiting')) return 'badge-warning';
            if (s.includes('fail') || s.includes('error') || s.includes('crash') || s.includes('notready')) return 'badge-error';
            return 'badge-info';
        }
        
        function toggleSelectAll(checkbox, prefix) {
            const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll(`input[type="checkbox"]`);
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => {
                if (cb !== checkbox) {
                    cb.checked = checkbox.checked;
                }
            });
            // Update selected items
            document.querySelectorAll(`input[type="checkbox"]:checked`).forEach(cb => {
                const name = cb.closest('tr')?.querySelector('td:nth-child(2)')?.textContent?.trim();
                if (name) {
                    state.selectedItems.add(prefix + ':' + name);
                }
            });
            updateMultiselectBar();
        }
        
        function toggleSelectItem(id) {
            if (state.selectedItems.has(id)) {
                state.selectedItems.delete(id);
            } else {
                state.selectedItems.add(id);
            }
            updateMultiselectBar();
        }
        
        function updateMultiselectBar() {
            const bar = document.getElementById('multiselect-bar');
            const count = document.getElementById('selected-count');
            if (state.selectedItems.size > 0) {
                bar.classList.add('show');
                count.textContent = state.selectedItems.size + ' selected';
            } else {
                bar.classList.remove('show');
            }
        }
        
        function clearSelection() {
            state.selectedItems.clear();
            updateMultiselectBar();
            document.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false);
        }
        
        function bulkRestart() {
            state.selectedItems.forEach(id => {
                const parts = id.split(':');
                const type = parts[0];
                const name = parts.slice(1).join(':');
                executeAction(type, name, 'restart');
            });
            setTimeout(() => clearSelection(), 1000);
        }
        
        function bulkDelete() {
            if (confirm(`Delete ${state.selectedItems.size} items?`)) {
                state.selectedItems.forEach(id => {
                    const parts = id.split(':');
                    const type = parts[0];
                    const name = parts.slice(1).join(':');
                    executeAction(type, name, 'delete');
                });
                setTimeout(() => clearSelection(), 1000);
            }
        }
        
        function toggleTerminal() {
            const terminal = document.getElementById('terminal');
            terminal.style.display = terminal.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
            if (terminal.style.display === 'block') {
                document.getElementById('terminal-output').innerHTML += `
                    <div class="terminal-line"><span class="terminal-prompt">$ </span>Terminal ready</div>
                `;
            }
        }
        
        function showAuditLog() {
            sendCommand('get_audit_log');
            document.getElementById('content-area').innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel">
                    <div class="panel-header">Audit Log</div>
                    <div class="panel-body" id="audit-content">
                        <div style="color:#6a7090;">Loading...</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        function restartSelected() {
            if (state.selectedItems.size === 0) {
                alert('Select items first by checking the boxes');
                return;
            }
            bulkRestart();
        }
        
        // ============================================================
        // KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS
        // ============================================================
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'k') {
                e.preventDefault();
                document.getElementById('global-search').focus();
            }
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                closeDrawer();
                document.getElementById('search-results').classList.remove('show');
                document.getElementById('alerts-dropdown').classList.remove('show');
            }
        });
        
        // Close search results on outside click
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (!e.target.closest('.search-container')) {
                document.getElementById('search-results').classList.remove('show');
            }
            if (!e.target.closest('.alert-bell') && !e.target.closest('.alerts-dropdown')) {
                document.getElementById('alerts-dropdown').classList.remove('show');
            }
        });
        
        // ============================================================
        // INITIALIZATION
        // ============================================================
        function init() {
            connectWebSocket();
            renderOverview();
            
            // Periodic refresh
            setInterval(() => {
                if (state.connected) {
                    sendCommand('get_metrics');
                    sendCommand('get_vms');
                    sendCommand('get_kubernetes');
                    sendCommand('get_events');
                    sendCommand('get_alerts');
                }
            }, 5000);
        }
        
        // Start the application
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
